\displaystyle(iv)\ \lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{g}{|p-q|}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}_{1}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\boldsymbol{\ell}_{1}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}_{1}^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{4}}=1,\quad p\neq q.

Lemma 4.2
.

Recall
\bar{C}_{0}
in (
3.45
) and
\bar{p}
in (
2.5
). For
\mathfrak{c}
suitably large, there hold

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}|p-q|\leq|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\frac{3}{2}|p-q|,\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3},

(4.1)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}|p^{0}|\leq|\bar{p}^{0}|\leq\frac{3}{2}|p^{0}|,\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3},

(4.2)

\displaystyle\frac{|p|}{2}-\bar{C}_{0}\leq|\bar{p}|\leq\frac{3|p|}{2}+\bar{C}_{0},\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3},

(4.3)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\leq\operatorname{det}\Big(\frac{\partial\bar{p}}{\partial p}\Big)\leq\frac{3}{2},\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3}.

(4.4)

Proof.

It follows from (
2.5
) that

\displaystyle\bar{p}-\bar{q}=p-q+\big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\big)\frac{u\cdot(p-q)}{|u|^{2}}u-\frac{p^{0}-q^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}u

(4.5)

and

\displaystyle\bar{p}^{0}=\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}p^{0}-\frac{u\cdot p}{\mathfrak{c}}=p^{0}+\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)p^{0}-\frac{u\cdot p}{\mathfrak{c}}.

(4.6)

For
\frac{\bar{C}_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\leq\frac{1}{4}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\big)\frac{u\cdot(p-q)}{|u|^{2}}u\Big|+\Big|\frac{p^{0}-q^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}u\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}(u^{0}+\mathfrak{c})}|p-q|+\frac{|u|}{\mathfrak{c}}|p-q|\leq\frac{1}{2}|p-q|

and

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)p^{0}-\frac{u\cdot p}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big|\leq\frac{|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}(u^{0}+\mathfrak{c})}p^{0}+\frac{|u|}{\mathfrak{c}}p^{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}p^{0},

which, together with (
4.5
) and (
4.6
), yield (
4.1
) and (
4.2
).

Observing

\displaystyle\bar{p}_{i}=p_{i}+\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)\frac{u_{i}}{|u|^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}u_{j}p_{j}-\frac{u_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}}p^{0},

(4.7)

we have

\displaystyle\Big|\big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\big)\frac{u\cdot p}{|u|^{2}}u\Big|+\Big|\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}u\Big|\leq\frac{|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}(u^{0}+\mathfrak{c})}|p|+\frac{|u|}{\mathfrak{c}}|p|+|u|\leq\frac{|p|}{2}+\bar{C}_{0},

which, together with (
4.7
), implies (
4.3
).

It follows from (
4.7
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\bar{p}_{i}}{\partial p_{j}}=\delta_{ij}+\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)\frac{u_{i}}{|u|^{2}}u_{j}-\frac{u_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}}\frac{p_{j}}{p^{0}}.

(4.8)

For
\mathfrak{c}
suitably large, it is clear that

\displaystyle\Big|\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)\frac{u_{i}}{|u|^{2}}u_{j}-\frac{u_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}}\frac{p_{j}}{p^{0}}\Big|\leq\frac{|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}(u^{0}+\mathfrak{c})}+\frac{|u|}{\mathfrak{c}}\leq\frac{1}{16},

which, together with (
4.8
), implies (
4.4
).
Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

Lemma 4.3
.

Recall
\bar{c}_{1}
in (
3.45
). Then there hold

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)\lesssim|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|e^{-2\bar{c}_{1}|\bar{p}|-2\bar{c}_{1}|\bar{q}|}\lesssim|p-q|e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|-\bar{c}_{1}|q|}

(4.9)

and

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)\lesssim\Big[\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}\Big]e^{-2\bar{c}_{1}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}\lesssim\Big[\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{1}{|p-q|}\Big]e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p-q|}.

(4.10)

Moreover, it holds that

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)\lesssim\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}\lesssim\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p-q|}.

(4.11)

Proof.

For any
p\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, it is clear that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}p^{0}=\mathfrak{c}^{2}(1-\sqrt{1+\frac{|p|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}})=-\frac{|p|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{1+\frac{|p|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}},

which yields

\displaystyle-\frac{|p|^{2}}{2}\leq\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}p^{0}\leq-\frac{|p|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{1+|p|^{2}}}=-\sqrt{1+|p|^{2}}+1\leq-|p|+1.

(4.12)

It follows from (
2.2
), Lemmas
4.1
–
4.2
and (
4.12
) that

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)

\displaystyle\lesssim|p-q|\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+u^{\mu}p_{\mu}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+u^{\mu}q_{\mu}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}

\displaystyle=|p-q|\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}

\displaystyle\lesssim|p-q|\exp{\Big(-\frac{|\bar{p}|+|\bar{q}|}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\lesssim|p-q|\exp{\Big(-\frac{|p|+|q|}{4T_{0}}\Big)}

and (
4.9
) follows.
For (
4.10
), observing
J_{2}(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}},\bar{\boldsymbol{j}})\leq J_{1}(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}},\bar{\boldsymbol{j}})
, we have from (
2.7
)–(
2.8
) that

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)\lesssim\mathfrak{c}\frac{s^{3/2}}{gp^{0}q^{0}}e^{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{T_{0}}}J_{1}(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}},\bar{\boldsymbol{j}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}\frac{s^{3/2}}{gp^{0}q^{0}}\frac{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}}{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2}}\left[1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}}\right]e^{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}{T_{0}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}\frac{s^{3/2}}{gp^{0}q^{0}}\frac{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}}{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2}}e^{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}{T_{0}}}.

It follows from Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{4}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{4}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\leq-\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{4}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{4}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{4}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{1+\frac{1}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}}\leq-\frac{1}{4}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{1+\frac{1}{4}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}}

\displaystyle=-\sqrt{1+\frac{1}{4}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}+1\leq-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{2}+1,

then we have

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathfrak{c}\frac{s^{3/2}}{gp^{0}q^{0}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{2T_{0}}\frac{1}{\frac{s\mathfrak{c}^{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{4g^{2}T_{0}^{2}}}e^{-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{2T_{0}}}\lesssim\frac{s^{1/2}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{g}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}e^{-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{2T_{0}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{g^{2}+4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{2T_{0}}}\lesssim\frac{(|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{2T_{0}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Big[\frac{1}{p^{0}}+\frac{1}{q^{0}}+\frac{1}{|p-q|}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{p^{0}}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{q^{0}}\Big)\Big]e^{-\frac{|p-q|}{4T_{0}}}\lesssim\Big[\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{1}{|p-q|}\Big]e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p-q|},

(4.13)

where we used the fact that both
s
and
g
are Lorentz invariant, i.e.,

\displaystyle s(p,q)=s(\bar{p},\bar{q}),\quad g(p,q)=g(\bar{p},\bar{q}).

Moreover, it follows from the fourth inequality of (
4
) that

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{2T_{0}}}\lesssim(|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+1)\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{2T_{0}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{4T_{0}}}\lesssim\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p-q|}.

Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

It follows from Lemmas
4.1
–
4.2
and (
4.12
) that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)\lesssim e^{-2\bar{c}_{1}|p|}.

(4.14)

Based on Lemma
4.3
, we can show the following key estimates.

Lemma 4.4
.

Let
\ell\geq 0,\,0\leq\varpi\leq\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{4}
and recall
w_{\ell}(p)
in (
1.27
). Then there hold

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)\frac{w_{\ell}(p)e^{\varpi|p|}}{w_{\ell}(q)e^{\varpi|q|}}dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|p|}

(4.15)

and

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)\frac{w_{\ell}(p)e^{\varpi|p|}}{w_{\ell}(q)e^{\varpi|q|}}dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{1}{1+|p|},\quad|p|\leq\mathfrak{c},\\
&\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}},\quad|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}.\end{aligned}\right.

(4.16)

Proof.

For
\ell>0
, notice that

\displaystyle\frac{(1+|p|^{2})^{\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{\varpi|p|}}{(1+|q|^{2})^{\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{\varpi|q|}}\lesssim(1+|p-q|^{2})^{\frac{\ell}{2}}e^{\varpi|p-q|},

which can be absorbed by the exponential part
e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{3}|p-q|}
of
k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)
and
k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)
. In the following, we mainly focus on the case of
\ell=0
.

For (
4.15
), it follows from (
4.9
) that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)dq\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|-\bar{c}_{1}|q|}dq\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p|-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|q|}dq\lesssim e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p|}\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|p|}.

Next we consider (
4.16
). If
|p|\leq 3\bar{C}_{0}
, we have from Lemmas
4.2
–
4.3
that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)dq\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p-q|}d\bar{q}\lesssim 1\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|p|}.

(4.17)

For
|p|\geq 3\bar{C}_{0}
, we divide the proof into three cases.

Case 1:

|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}d\bar{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}d\bar{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\lesssim\frac{1}{1+\mathfrak{c}}.

(4.18)

Case 2:

|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}
. For any
p,q\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, it is direct to check that

\displaystyle g^{2}-|p-q|^{2}

\displaystyle=2p^{0}q^{0}-2\mathfrak{c}^{2}-2p\cdot q-|p-q|^{2}

\displaystyle=2\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})+|p|^{2}|q|^{2}}{p^{0}q^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}}-(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{p^{0}q^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big\{\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})+|p|^{2}|q|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})(p^{0}q^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p^{0}q^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\{(\mathfrak{c}^{2}-p^{0}q^{0})(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})+2|p|^{2}|q|^{2}\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p^{0}q^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big\{-\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})+|p|^{2}|q|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+p^{0}q^{0}}(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})+2|p|^{2}|q|^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(q^{0}|p|^{2}-p^{0}|q|^{2})^{2}}{(p^{0}q^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{2}},

which, together with Lemma
4.1
, yields that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{4}-(\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2})}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}=\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{4}-\frac{s\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{4g^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+\frac{\sqrt{s}\mathfrak{c}}{2g}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}=\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\frac{s}{4g^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\frac{g^{2}+4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{4g^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}=\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}\Big\{-\frac{1}{4}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{g^{2}}(g^{2}-|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2})\Big\}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{4}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}-\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{g^{2}}\frac{(\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}|^{2}-\bar{p}^{0}|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{(\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{2}}.

(4.19)

Hence, it follows from (
4
) and (
4
) that

\displaystyle\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{\frac{1}{2T_{0}}(\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}})}dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{M(\bar{p},\bar{q})}\exp{\Big(-\frac{1}{8T_{0}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}\Big)}d\bar{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{M(\bar{p},\bar{q})}d\bar{q},

(4.20)

where we made a change of variables
q\rightarrow\bar{q}
and

\displaystyle M(\bar{p},\bar{q}):=-\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{g^{2}}\frac{(\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}|^{2}-\bar{p}^{0}|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{(\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{2}}\frac{1}{2T_{0}}.

(4.21)

Noting
|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}
, one has
|\bar{p}|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}
and so

\displaystyle\bar{p}^{0}=\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}}\lesssim\mathfrak{c},\quad\bar{q}^{0}=\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}}\leq\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+2|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}+2|\bar{p}|^{2}}\lesssim\mathfrak{c},

(4.22)

which yields that

\displaystyle(1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g})g^{2}(\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\sqrt{1+\frac{g^{2}}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}})|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}(\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{4}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}.

A direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle\bar{p}^{0}|\bar{q}|^{2}-\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{c}(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})+(\bar{p}^{0}-\mathfrak{c})|\bar{q}|^{2}-(\bar{q}^{0}-\mathfrak{c})|\bar{p}|^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{c}(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})+\frac{|\bar{p}|^{2}|\bar{q}|^{2}}{\bar{p}^{0}+\mathfrak{c}}-\frac{|\bar{p}|^{2}|\bar{q}|^{2}}{\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c}}

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{c}(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})+\frac{|\bar{p}|^{2}|\bar{q}|^{2}}{(\bar{p}^{0}+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c})}(\bar{q}^{0}-\bar{p}^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{c}(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})+\frac{|\bar{p}|^{2}|\bar{q}|^{2}(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})}{(\bar{p}^{0}+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}.

(4.23)

Thus, in view of (
3.45
) and (
4.22
)–(
4
), there exists a positive constant
\alpha_{0}
which is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
such that

\displaystyle M(\bar{p},\bar{q})

\displaystyle\leq-\alpha_{0}\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\left(\mathfrak{c}(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})+\frac{|\bar{p}|^{2}|\bar{q}|^{2}(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})}{(\bar{p}^{0}+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\alpha_{0}\frac{(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})^{2}}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}.

(4.24)

Combining (
4
) and (
4
), one has that

\displaystyle\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\alpha_{0}\frac{(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})^{2}}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}}d\bar{q}.

By taking similar arguments as in
[
17
, Lemma 3.3.1]
(see also Case 3 below), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)dq\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|\bar{p}|}\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|p|},\quad\text{for}\ |p|\leq\mathfrak{c}.

(4.25)

Case 3:

|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}
. It is clear that
|\bar{p}|\gtrsim\mathfrak{c}
. Noting

\displaystyle|\bar{q}|\leq|\bar{q}-\bar{p}|+|\bar{p}|\lesssim|\bar{p}|,\quad|\bar{q}|\geq|\bar{p}|-|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\gtrsim|\bar{p}|,

then we have

\displaystyle|\bar{p}|\cong|\bar{q}|,\quad\bar{p}^{0}\cong\bar{q}^{0}.

Hence it is clear that

\displaystyle(1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g})g^{2}(\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\sqrt{1+\frac{g^{2}}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}})|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}(\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}(\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2})^{2}.

(4.26)

For
|\bar{p}|\gtrsim\mathfrak{c}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{c}+\frac{|\bar{p}|^{2}|\bar{q}|^{2}}{(\bar{p}^{0}+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{q}^{0}+\mathfrak{c})(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}\cong\mathfrak{c}+\frac{|\bar{p}|^{4}}{(\bar{p}^{0})^{3}}\cong\mathfrak{c}+\frac{|\bar{p}|^{4}}{(\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\cong\mathfrak{c}+|\bar{p}|,

which, together with (
4
), yields that

\displaystyle(\bar{p}^{0}|\bar{q}|^{2}-\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}|^{2})^{2}\cong(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})^{2}(\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}).

(4.27)

Combining (
4.21
), (
4
) and (
4.27
), for some positive constant
\alpha_{1}
which is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
, we have

\displaystyle M(\bar{p},\bar{q})\leq-\alpha_{1}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}}\frac{(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})^{2}}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}.

(4.28)

Hence, for
|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}
, it follows from (
4
) and (
4.28
) that

\displaystyle\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{M(\bar{p},\bar{q})}d\bar{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}e^{-\alpha_{1}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}}\frac{(|\bar{q}|^{2}-|\bar{p}|^{2})^{2}}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}}d\bar{q}.

Following the arguments as in
[
17
, Lemma 3.3.1]
, we can make a change of variables

\displaystyle|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|=r,\quad(\bar{q}-\bar{p})\cdot\bar{p}=|\bar{p}|r\cos\theta,\quad 0\leq r<\infty,\ 0\leq\theta\leq\pi,

which yields that

\displaystyle|\bar{q}|^{2}=|\bar{q}-\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+2(\bar{q}-\bar{p})\cdot\bar{p}=r^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+2r|\bar{p}|\cos\theta.

Denoting
\alpha_{2}^{2}:=\alpha_{1}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}}
and
u=\alpha_{2}(r+2|\bar{p}|\cos\theta)
, one has

\displaystyle\int_{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{\infty}re^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}r}dr\int_{0}^{\pi}e^{-\alpha_{2}^{2}(r+2|\bar{p}|\cos\theta)^{2}}\sin\theta d\theta

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\alpha_{2}|\bar{p}|}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}e^{-u^{2}}du\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}}}{\mathfrak{c}|\bar{p}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}},

which, together with (
4.17
), (
4
), (
4.25
), yields (
4.16
). Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

By similar arguments as in Lemma
4.4
, one can also obtain

Lemma 4.5
.

There hold

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\mathfrak{c}1}^{2}(p,q)\Big(\frac{w_{\ell}(p)}{w_{\ell}(q)}\Big)^{2}dq\lesssim\frac{1}{1+|p|}

and

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\mathfrak{c}2}^{2}(p,q)\Big(\frac{w_{\ell}(p)}{w_{\ell}(q)}\Big)^{2}dq\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{1}{1+|p|},\quad|p|\leq\mathfrak{c},\\
&\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}},\quad|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}.\end{aligned}\right.

Recall
k_{\mathfrak{c}}(p,q)=k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)-k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)
in (
2.9
) and denote

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}w}(p,q):=k_{\mathfrak{c}}(p,q)\frac{w_{\ell}(p)}{w_{\ell}(q)}.

By similar arguments as in Lemma
4.4
, one can also obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k_{\mathfrak{c}w}(p,q)e^{\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{4}|p-q|}dq\lesssim\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{1}{1+|p|},\quad\text{for}\ |p|\leq\mathfrak{c},\\
&\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}},\quad\text{for}\ |p|\geq\mathfrak{c}.\end{aligned}\right.

Next we estimate the collision frequency
\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)
.

Lemma 4.6
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)\cong\left\{\begin{aligned} &1+|p|,\quad|p|\leq\mathfrak{c},\\
&\mathfrak{c},\quad|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}.\end{aligned}\right.

(4.29)

Proof.

Recall

\displaystyle\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{4}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)d\omega dq.

Since the proof is complicated, we split it into four cases.

Case 1:

|q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
. Using Lemma
4.1
and (
4.14
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{4}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)d\omega dq\lesssim\int_{|q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\mathfrak{c}e^{-2\bar{c}_{1}|q|}dq\lesssim e^{-\bar{c}_{1}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}.

(4.30)

Case 2:

|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{3}{8}}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{4p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{n_{0}\gamma}{4\pi\mathfrak{c}^{3}K_{2}(\gamma)}\exp{\Big(\frac{u^{\mu}q_{\mu}}{T_{0}}\Big)}d\omega dq

\displaystyle=\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{4p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+O(\gamma^{-1}))\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{T_{0}}\Big)}d\omega dq

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{4p^{0}q^{0}}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{T_{0}}\Big)}d\omega dq\cdot O(\gamma^{-1})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{4p^{0}q^{0}}-\frac{|p-q|}{2}\Big)\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{T_{0}}\Big)}d\omega dq

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{|p-q|}{2}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{T_{0}}\Big)}d\omega dq

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{H}_{1}+\mathcal{H}_{2}+\mathcal{H}_{3}.

(4.31)

It is clear that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}_{1}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|p-q|e^{-2\bar{c}_{1}|q|}dq\cong\frac{1+|p|}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{13}{8}}.

(4.32)

Using Lemma
4.2
, (
3.45
) and (
4.12
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{3}\gtrsim\int_{|\bar{q}|\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|\exp{\Big(-\frac{|\bar{q}|^{2}}{8\bar{c}_{1}}\Big)}d\bar{q}\cong 1+|\bar{p}|\gtrsim 1+|p|.

(4.33)

For
\mathcal{H}_{2}
, notice that

\displaystyle g^{2}

\displaystyle=2p^{0}q^{0}-2p\cdot q-2\mathfrak{c}^{2}=|p-q|^{2}+2p^{0}q^{0}-2\mathfrak{c}^{2}-|p|^{2}-|q|^{2}

\displaystyle=|p-q|^{2}+\frac{4(|p|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})(|q|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})-(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})^{2}}{2p^{0}q^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})}

\displaystyle=|p-q|^{2}-\frac{(|p|^{2}-|q|^{2})^{2}}{2p^{0}q^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})},

(4.34)

then one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{4p^{0}q^{0}}-\frac{|p-q|}{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4p^{0}q^{0}}\{\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}-2p^{0}q^{0}|p-q|\}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}g^{2}(g^{2}+4\mathfrak{c}^{2})-4|p-q|^{2}(|p|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})(|q|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})}{4p^{0}q^{0}(\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}+2p^{0}q^{0}|p-q|)}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{4}(g^{2}-|p-q|^{2})+\mathfrak{c}^{2}g^{4}-4|p-q|^{2}\{|p|^{2}|q|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})\}}{4p^{0}q^{0}(\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}+2p^{0}q^{0}|p-q|)}

\displaystyle\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{7}{8}}),

which implies that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{H}_{2}|\lesssim\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{7}{8}}e^{-2\bar{c}_{1}|q|}dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{7}{8}}.

(4.35)

It follows from (
4
)–(
4.33
) and (
4.35
) that

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{4}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)d\omega dq\cong 1+|p|.

(4.36)

Case 3:

|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
\mathfrak{c}\geq|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{3}{8}}
.
It follows from Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle g\geq\frac{\mathfrak{c}|p-q|}{\sqrt{p^{0}q^{0}}}\gtrsim\frac{\mathfrak{c}|p|}{\mathfrak{c}}=|p|

and

\displaystyle g\leq|p-q|\lesssim|p|,

which yields that
g\cong|p|
. Thus we have

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{4}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)d\omega dq\cong\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}|p|\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{T_{0}}\Big)}d\omega dq\cong 1+|p|.

(4.37)

Case 4:

|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}
. It is obvious that

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{4}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)d\omega dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\mathfrak{c}e^{-2\bar{c}_{1}|q|}dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}.

(4.38)

On the other hand, since
|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}
, one has

\displaystyle g\geq\frac{\mathfrak{c}|p-q|}{\sqrt{p^{0}q^{0}}}\gtrsim\frac{\mathfrak{c}|p|}{(|p|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}}}\gtrsim\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}|p|}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{4}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)d\omega dq

\displaystyle\gtrsim\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}|p|}\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+\mathfrak{c}|p|}}{p^{0}}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{T_{0}}\Big)}dq\gtrsim\mathfrak{c}.

(4.39)

It follows from (
4.38
) and (
4.39
) that

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{4}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)d\omega dq

\displaystyle\cong\mathfrak{c}.

(4.40)

Combining (
4.30
), (
4.36
), (
4.37
) and (
4.40
), we conclude (
4.29
). Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

Remark 4.7
.

By similar arguments as in Lemma
4.6
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\alpha}(q)d\omega dq

\displaystyle\cong\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p),\quad\text{for}\ \alpha>0.

(4.41)

4.1.
Uniform-in-
\mathfrak{c}
coercivity estimate on
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}

In this subsection, we shall derive a uniform-in-
\mathfrak{c}
coercivity estimate for the linearized relativistic collision operator
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}
.
For later use, we denote

\displaystyle k_{1}(p,q)

\displaystyle:=2\pi|p-q|\frac{\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}-\frac{|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}},

(4.42)

\displaystyle k_{2}(p,q)

\displaystyle:=\frac{2}{|p-q|}\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{2\pi\theta}}e^{-\frac{|p-q|^{2}}{8\theta}-\frac{(|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})^{2}}{8\theta|p-q|^{2}}},

(4.43)

which are indeed the corresponding kernels of Newtonian Boltzmann equation.

Lemma 4.8
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)-k_{1}(p,q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}},\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3}.

(4.44)

Proof.

We remark that throughout the proof, we make no attempt to be optimal in our estimates. We split the proof into three cases.

Case 1.

|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
. It follows from (
3.45
), (
4.42
) and Lemma
4.2
that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)-k_{1}(p,q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|-\bar{c}_{1}|q|}dq+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|e^{-\frac{|p|^{2}}{8\theta}-\frac{|q|^{2}}{8\theta}}dq

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}+e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{2}}{4}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

(4.45)

Case 2.

|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
. Similar to (
4.1
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)-k_{1}(p,q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|p-q|e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|-\bar{c}_{1}|q|}dq+\int_{|q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|p-q|e^{-\frac{|p|^{2}}{8\theta}-\frac{|q|^{2}}{8\theta}}dq

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}+e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{2}}{4}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

(4.46)

Case 3.

|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
. Recall that

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{n_{0}}{4\pi\mathfrak{c}T_{0}K_{2}(\gamma)}\exp{\Big(\frac{u^{\mu}p_{\mu}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\exp{\Big(\frac{u^{\mu}q_{\mu}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+O(\gamma^{-1}))\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+u^{\mu}p_{\mu}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+u^{\mu}q_{\mu}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+O(\gamma^{-1}))\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}.

Then we have

\displaystyle|k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)-k_{1}(p,q)|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\pi\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\cdot O(\gamma^{-1})

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big|\frac{\pi\mathfrak{c}g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}-2\pi|p-q|\Big|\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\pi|p-q|\Big|\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big|\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}\exp{\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\pi|p-q|\frac{\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{-\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}-\frac{|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}}\Big|\exp{\Big(\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}+\frac{|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}}{2T_{0}}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big)}-1\Big|

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{D}_{1}+\mathcal{D}_{2}+\mathcal{D}_{3}+\mathcal{D}_{4}.

(4.47)

It is clear that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{D}_{1}|\lesssim\frac{|p-q|}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|-\bar{c}_{1}|q|},

which implies that

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\mathcal{D}_{1}(q,p)|dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-2}.

(4.48)

For
\mathcal{D}_{2}
, we notice that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{c}\sqrt{s}}{2p^{0}q^{0}}-1

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathfrak{c}\sqrt{s}-2p^{0}q^{0}}{2p^{0}q^{0}}=\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}g^{2}-4\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})-4|p|^{2}|q|^{2}}{2p^{0}q^{0}(\mathfrak{c}\sqrt{s}+2p^{0}q^{0})}\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}).

(4.49)

It follows from (
4
) that

\displaystyle g^{2}-|p-q|^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(|p|^{2}-|q|^{2})^{2}}{2p^{0}q^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|p|^{2}+|q|^{2})}\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}),

(4.50)

which yields that

\displaystyle|g-|p-q||=\frac{|g^{2}-|p-q|^{2}|}{g+|p-q|}\lesssim\frac{O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}})}{g+|p-q|}\lesssim\frac{O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}})}{|p-q|}.

(4.51)

Using (
4.49
) and (
4.51
), one has

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{2}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}-|p-q|\Big|

\displaystyle\leq|g(\frac{\mathfrak{c}\sqrt{s}}{2p^{0}q^{0}}-1)|+|g-|p-q||

\displaystyle\lesssim(g+\frac{1}{|p-q|})\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}\lesssim(|p-q|+\frac{1}{|p-q|})\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}},

which implies that

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\mathcal{D}_{2}(q,p)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\Big|\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{2}\frac{g\sqrt{s}}{p^{0}q^{0}}-|p-q|\Big|e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|-\bar{c}_{1}|q|}dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\Big(|p-q|+\frac{1}{|p-q|}\Big)e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|-\bar{c}_{1}|q|}\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

(4.52)

For
\mathcal{D}_{3}
, it follows from Proposition
3.8
that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big|\lesssim|T_{0}-\theta|+|n_{0}-\rho|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-2},

(4.53)

which yields that

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\mathcal{D}_{3}(q,p)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-2}\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|p-q|e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|-\bar{c}_{1}|q|}\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-2}.

(4.54)

For
\mathcal{D}_{4}
, a direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}}{2T_{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta T_{0}}(T_{0}-\theta)+\frac{1}{4T_{0}}(|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}+2\mathfrak{c}^{2}-2\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta T_{0}}(T_{0}-\theta)+\frac{1}{4T_{0}}\Big[\frac{|p|^{4}}{(p^{0}+\mathfrak{c})^{2}}+2p\cdot(u-\mathfrak{u})+(|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|u|^{2})\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{4T_{0}}\Big[\frac{|u|^{4}}{(u^{0}+\mathfrak{c})^{2}}-2\frac{|p|^{2}|u|^{2}}{(u^{0}+\mathfrak{c})(p^{0}+\mathfrak{c})}\Big],

(4.55)

which implies that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

(4.56)

Similarly, one has

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{4\theta}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\mathfrak{c}\bar{q}^{0}}{2T_{0}}\Big|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\mathcal{D}_{4}(q,p)|dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|p-q|e^{-\frac{|p|^{2}}{8\theta}-\frac{|q|^{2}}{8\theta}}dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

(4.57)

Combining (
4.1
), (
4.48
), (
4.1
), (
4.54
) and (
4.57
), we have that

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|k_{\mathfrak{c}1}(p,q)-k_{1}(p,q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}},\quad|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}.

(4.58)

Hence, we conclude (
4.44
) from (
4.1
), (
4.1
) and (
4.58
). Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

Lemma 4.9
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)-k_{2}(p,q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{8}},\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3}.

Proof.

Since the proof is complicated, we split the proof into three cases.

Case 1.

|p-q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
. It follows from (
3.45
), (
4.11
) and (
4.43
) that

\displaystyle\int_{|p-q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)-k_{2}(p,q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{|p-q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p-q|}dq+\int_{|p-q|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{|p-q|^{2}}{8\theta}}dq

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{4}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}+e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{2}}{4}\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}}\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{8}}.

(4.59)

Case 2.

|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{3}{8}}
. By Lemma
4.4
and similar arguments for
k_{2}(p,q)
as in
[
17
, Lemma 3.3.1]
, one has

\displaystyle\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)-k_{2}(p,q)|dq\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{1}{1+|p|}+\frac{1}{1+|p-\mathfrak{u}|}\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{8}}.

(4.60)

Case 3.

|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
and
|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{3}{8}}
. In this case, we have
|q|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{3}{8}}
. Recall that

\displaystyle k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)=\frac{\mathfrak{c}\pi s^{\frac{3}{2}}}{4gp^{0}q^{0}}\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}(1+O(\gamma^{-1}))\frac{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\sqrt{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2}}+\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}+(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})}{(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}{T_{0}}}.

Then one has

\displaystyle|k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)-k_{2}(p,q)|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathfrak{c}\pi s^{\frac{3}{2}}}{4gp^{0}q^{0}}\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\sqrt{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2}}+\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}+(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})}{(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}{T_{0}}}\cdot O(\gamma^{-1})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\mathfrak{c}\pi s^{\frac{3}{2}}}{4gp^{0}q^{0}}\Big|\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big|\frac{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\sqrt{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2}}+\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}+(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})}{(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}e^{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}{T_{0}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{4\pi\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big|\frac{\mathfrak{c}s^{\frac{3}{2}}}{16gp^{0}q^{0}}\frac{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\sqrt{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2}}+\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}+(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})}{(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\theta}{|p-q|}\Big|e^{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}{T_{0}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2}{|p-q|}\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{2\pi\theta}}e^{-\frac{|p-q|^{2}}{8\theta}-\frac{(|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})^{2}}{8\theta|p-q|^{2}}}\Big|e^{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}{T_{0}}+\frac{|p-q|^{2}}{8\theta}+\frac{(|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})^{2}}{8\theta|p-q|^{2}}}-1\Big|

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{E}_{1}+\mathcal{E}_{2}+\mathcal{E}_{3}+\mathcal{E}_{4}.

(4.61)

It follows from (
4.11
) that

\displaystyle\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\mathcal{E}_{1}|dq\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p-q|}dq\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}.

(4.62)

By (
4.53
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\mathcal{E}_{2}|dq\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p-q|}dq\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}.

(4.63)

We next focus on
\mathcal{E}_{3}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{c}s^{\frac{3}{2}}}{16gp^{0}q^{0}}\frac{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\sqrt{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2}}+\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}+(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})}{(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\theta}{|p-q|}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\frac{g^{2}T_{0}^{3}}{p^{0}q^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{3}}\Big(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\sqrt{\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2}}+\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}+(\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}^{2}-\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}^{2})\Big)-\frac{\theta}{|p-q|}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\frac{g^{2}}{p^{0}q^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{3}}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\sqrt{s}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{4g}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|T_{0}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}s}{4g^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}T_{0}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{2}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})T_{0}^{2}\Big)-\frac{\theta}{|p-q|}

\displaystyle=\Big\{\frac{1}{2}\frac{g^{2}}{\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{3}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{s}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{4g}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\frac{s}{4g^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}\Big)\theta-\frac{\theta}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}\Big\}+\frac{g^{2}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{4\mathfrak{c}p^{0}q^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{3}}T_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\frac{g^{2}}{p^{0}q^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{3}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{s}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{4g}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\frac{s}{4g^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}\Big)(T_{0}-\theta)+\Big(\frac{\theta}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}-\frac{\theta}{|p-q|}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\frac{g^{2}}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{3}}\Big(\frac{1}{p^{0}q^{0}}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}}\Big)\Big(\frac{\sqrt{s}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{4g}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\frac{s}{4g^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}\Big)\theta

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{E}_{31}+\mathcal{E}_{32}+\mathcal{E}_{33}+\mathcal{E}_{34}+\mathcal{E}_{35}.

(4.64)

A direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{32}|+|\mathcal{E}_{33}|+|\mathcal{E}_{34}|+|\mathcal{E}_{35}|\lesssim\frac{1}{|p-q|}\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{13}{8}}.

(4.65)

For
\mathcal{E}_{31}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{E}_{31}}{\theta}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{g^{2}}{\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{s}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{4g}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\frac{s}{4g^{2}}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}\Big)-1

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\Big(\frac{\sqrt{s}g(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}-1\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\Big(\frac{s}{4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}}-1\Big)

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{E}_{311}+\mathcal{E}_{312}.

For
\mathcal{E}_{312}
, we notice that

\displaystyle\frac{s}{4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}}-1

\displaystyle=\frac{s-4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}}{4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}}=\frac{(g^{2}+4\mathfrak{c}^{2})^{2}-16(\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2})(\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2})}{4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}(s+4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0})}

\displaystyle=\frac{g^{4}+8g^{2}\mathfrak{c}^{2}-16\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2})-16|\bar{p}|^{2}|\bar{q}|^{2}}{4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}(s+4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0})}

\displaystyle\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}).

For
\mathcal{E}_{311}
, it is clear that

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{s}g(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})}{4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}-1

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{s}g(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})-4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{4\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{s}g-2\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{4\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}+\frac{\sqrt{s}g-2\bar{p}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{4p^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}.

Due to (
4.50
), one has

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{s}g-2\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{4\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}=

\displaystyle\frac{(g^{2}+4\mathfrak{c}^{2})g^{2}-4(|\bar{q}|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{4\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|(\sqrt{s}g+2\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}(g^{2}-|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2})+g^{4}-4|\bar{q}|^{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{4\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|(\sqrt{s}g+2\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{4\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|(\sqrt{s}g+2\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{g^{4}-4|\bar{q}|^{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{4\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|(\sqrt{s}g+2\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}})

and

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{s}g-2\bar{p}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{4\bar{p}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}).

Thus we can obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{31}|\lesssim\frac{1}{|p-q|}\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}.

(4.66)

Combining (
4.65
) and (
4.66
), one obtains, for
|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{3}{8}}
, that

\displaystyle\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\mathcal{E}_{3}|dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|p-q|}dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}.

(4.67)

Next, we consider
\mathcal{E}_{4}
. It follows from (
4
) and (
4
) that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}}{T_{0}}+\frac{|p-q|^{2}}{8\theta}+\frac{(|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})^{2}}{8\theta|p-q|^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T_{0}}\Big[\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}+\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8}+\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\Big]+\Big[\frac{|p-q|^{2}}{8\theta}-\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8T_{0}}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\Big[\frac{(|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})^{2}}{8\theta|p-q|^{2}}-\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8T_{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\Big]

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{G}_{1}+\mathcal{G}_{2}+\mathcal{G}_{3}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{2}
, using (
2.5
) and Proposition
3.8
, one has

\displaystyle|\mathcal{G}_{2}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\theta}\Big||p-q|^{2}-|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}\Big|+|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}\frac{|\theta-T_{0}|}{\theta T_{0}}\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{7}{8}}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{3}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\theta}(|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})^{2}\Big[\frac{1}{|p-q|^{2}}-\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{8\theta}\frac{1}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\Big[(|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-|q-\mathfrak{u}|^{2})^{2}-(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\frac{T_{0}-\theta}{8\theta T_{0}}

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{G}_{31}+\mathcal{G}_{32}+\mathcal{G}_{33}.

Applying (
2.5
) and Proposition
3.8
again, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{G}_{31}|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{7}{8}},\quad|\mathcal{G}_{32}|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}},\quad|\mathcal{G}_{33}|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}},

which yields that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{G}_{3}|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{7}{8}}.

We finally focus on
\mathcal{G}_{1}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8}+\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}-\sqrt{\boldsymbol{\ell}^{2}-\boldsymbol{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\Big[\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}-\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{g^{2}}\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2})}\Big]

\displaystyle\quad+\Big[\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8}-\frac{1}{4}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}\Big]

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{G}_{11}+\mathcal{G}_{12}.

(4.68)

For
\mathcal{G}_{12}
, we have from (
4
) that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{12}=

\displaystyle\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8}\Big(1-\frac{2}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8}\frac{\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}-1}{\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}+1}=\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8}\frac{\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|-2\mathfrak{c}g}{\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+2\mathfrak{c}g}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8}\frac{g^{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}+4\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}-g^{2})}{(\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+2\mathfrak{c}g)^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{8}\Big\{\frac{g^{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{(\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+2\mathfrak{c}g)^{2}}+\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{(\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+2\mathfrak{c}g)^{2}}\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2})}\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle O(\mathfrak{c}^{-1}).

(4.69)

For
\mathcal{G}_{11}
, it can be written as

\displaystyle\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\Big\{1-\frac{1}{1+\sqrt{\frac{s}{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}}}\frac{|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{g}}\frac{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{g^{2}}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}{2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2})}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\frac{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))-16\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle=\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\frac{(4\mathfrak{c}^{3}g^{2}-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2})+(4\mathfrak{c}g^{2}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2})+(2\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g\mathfrak{c}^{2}-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2})}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}\frac{(2\sqrt{s}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2})+(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2})}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle:=\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{8|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}(\mathcal{G}_{111}+\mathcal{G}_{112}+\mathcal{G}_{113}+\mathcal{G}_{114}+\mathcal{G}_{115}).

(4.70)

We have from (
4
) that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{111}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{3}g^{2}-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle=\frac{-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}\frac{(|\bar{p}|^{2}-|\bar{q}|^{2})^{2}}{2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2})}

\displaystyle\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}),

(4.71)

where we have used
g^{2}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}\geq\mathfrak{c}^{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}
. Similarly, one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{112}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\mathfrak{c}g^{2}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle=\frac{-4\mathfrak{c}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}(|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}-g^{2})+4\mathfrak{c}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}(\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}-\mathfrak{c}^{2})}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}),

(4.72)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{113}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g\mathfrak{c}^{2}-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle=\frac{-2\mathfrak{c}^{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}(2\mathfrak{c}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|-\sqrt{s}g)

\displaystyle=\frac{-2\mathfrak{c}^{2}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}(|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}-g^{2})-g^{4}}{2\mathfrak{c}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\sqrt{s}g}

\displaystyle\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}),

(4.73)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{114}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{s}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g-4\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle=\frac{-2|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}(2\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|-\sqrt{s}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}g)

\displaystyle=\frac{-2|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{6}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}-s(\bar{p}^{0})^{2}(\bar{q}^{0})^{2}g^{2}}{2\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\sqrt{s}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}g}

\displaystyle=\frac{-2|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big\{\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{6}(|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|^{2}-g^{2})-g^{4}(|\bar{p}|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})(|\bar{q}|^{2}+\mathfrak{c}^{2})}{2\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\sqrt{s}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}g}-\frac{4\mathfrak{c}^{2}g^{2}(|\bar{p}|^{2}\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}|\bar{q}|^{2})}{2\mathfrak{c}^{3}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|+\sqrt{s}\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}g}\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}})

(4.74)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{115}

\displaystyle=\frac{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2})}{(2\mathfrak{c}g^{2}+\sqrt{s}|\bar{p}-\bar{q}|g)(2\bar{p}^{0}\bar{q}^{0}+(2\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|\bar{p}|^{2}+|\bar{q}|^{2}))}\lesssim O(\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{5}{4}}).

(4.75)

Combining (
4.1
)–(
4.75
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{G}_{11}|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{1}{2}},

which, together with (
4.1
)–(
4.1
), yields that

\displaystyle\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}|\mathcal{E}_{4}|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{|p-q|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{|p-q|^{2}}{8\theta}-\frac{(|p|^{2}-|q|^{2})^{2}}{8\theta|p-q|^{2}}}dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.76)

Combining (
4.1
)–(
4.63
), (
4.67
) and (
4.76
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|k_{\mathfrak{c}2}(p,q)-k_{2}(p,q)|dq\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{8}},\quad p\in\mathbb{R}.

Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

Denote
\overline{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathfrak{c}}
as the local Maxwellian in the rest frame where
(u^{0},u^{1},u^{2},u^{3})^{t}=(\mathfrak{c},0,0,0)^{t}
:

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p):=\frac{n_{0}\gamma}{4\pi\mathfrak{c}^{3}K_{2}(\gamma)}\exp\Big\{\frac{-\mathfrak{c}p^{0}}{T_{0}}\Big\}.

Define the third momentum

\displaystyle T^{\alpha\beta\gamma}[\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}]:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{p^{\alpha}p^{\beta}p^{\gamma}}{p^{0}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp,\quad\overline{T}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{p^{\alpha}p^{\beta}p^{\gamma}}{p^{0}}\overline{\mathbf{M}}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp.

We first give the expression of
\overline{T}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}
which can be proved directly and we omit the details here for brevity.

Lemma 4.10
.

Let
i,j,k\in\{1,2,3\}
. For the third momentum
\overline{T}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}
which corresponds to
T^{\alpha\beta\gamma}[\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}]
in the rest frame, there hold

\displaystyle\overline{T}^{000}

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{0}\mathfrak{c}^{2}\left[3K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right]}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)},

\displaystyle\overline{T}^{0ii}

\displaystyle=\overline{T}^{ii0}=\overline{T}^{i0i}=\frac{n_{0}\mathfrak{c}^{2}K_{3}(\gamma)}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)},

\displaystyle\overline{T}^{\alpha\beta\gamma}

\displaystyle=0,\quad\text{ if }(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)\neq(0,0,0),(0,i,i),(i,i,0),(i,0,i).

Recalling the Lorentz transformation in (
2.4
) and observing

T^{\alpha\beta\gamma}[\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}]=\bar{\Lambda}_{\alpha^{\prime}}^{\alpha}\bar{\Lambda}_{\beta^{\prime}}^{\beta}\bar{\Lambda}_{\gamma^{\prime}}^{\gamma}\overline{T}^{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}},

we can obtain the expression of
T^{\alpha\beta\gamma}[\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}]
from Lemma
4.10
.

Lemma 4.11
.

For
i,j,k\in\{1,2,3\}
, there hold

\displaystyle T^{000}[\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}]=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(3K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{3}+3K_{3}(\gamma)u^{0}|u|^{2}\right],

\displaystyle T^{00i}[\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}]=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(5K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}u_{i}+K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}u_{i}\right],

\displaystyle T^{0ij}[\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}]=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)u^{0}u_{i}u_{j}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}K_{3}(\gamma)u^{0}\delta_{ij}\right],

\displaystyle T^{ijk}[\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}]=

\displaystyle\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big[(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma))u_{i}u_{j}u_{k}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}K_{3}(\gamma)\left(u_{i}\delta_{jk}+u_{j}\delta_{ik}+u_{k}\delta_{ij}\right)\Big].

Since we have not found a direct reference which gives the orthonormal basis of
\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}
, so we present details of calculation in the appendix for completeness though it is somehow routine. Indeed, the orthonormal basis of
\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}
for the relativistic Boltzmann equation has the form

\displaystyle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{0}=\mathfrak{a}_{0}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}},\quad\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{j}=\frac{p_{j}-\mathfrak{a}_{j}}{\mathfrak{b}_{j}}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\ (j=1,2,3),\quad\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4}=\frac{p^{0}/\mathfrak{c}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}p_{i}+\mathfrak{e}}{\zeta}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}},

(4.77)

where
\mathfrak{a}_{\alpha}\ (\alpha=0,1,2,3)
,
\mathfrak{b}_{j}\ (j=1,2,3)
,
\lambda_{i}\ (i=1,2,3)
and
\mathfrak{e}
are all given in the appendix.

In the following lemma, we shall show that, as
\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty
, the relativistic orthonormal basis in (
4.77
) converges to the following Newtonian orthonormal basis

\displaystyle\chi_{0}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\rho}}\sqrt{\mu},\quad\chi_{j}=\frac{p_{j}-\mathfrak{u}_{j}}{\sqrt{\rho\theta}}\sqrt{\mu}\ (j=1,2,3),\quad\chi_{4}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{6\rho}}\Big(\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{\theta}-3\Big)\sqrt{\mu},

(4.78)

where
\mu(t,x,p)
is defined by (
1.30
).

Lemma 4.12
.

For any fixed
p\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{\alpha}=\chi_{\alpha},\quad\alpha=0,1,\cdots,4.

Proof.

In view of Proposition
3.8
, one has

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)=\mu(p),\quad\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}I^{0}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}=\rho.

Then we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\mathfrak{a}_{0}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{I^{0}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\rho}},

which implies that
\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{0}=\chi_{0}
.

For
j=1,2,3
, a direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}T^{0j}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}u^{0}u_{j}=\rho\mathfrak{u}_{j}

(4.79)

and

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}T^{0jj}

\displaystyle=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)u^{0}u^{2}_{j}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}K_{3}(\gamma)u^{0}\right]=\rho\mathfrak{u}^{2}_{j}+\rho\theta.

Thus one has

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\mathfrak{a}_{j}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{T^{0j}}{I_{0}}=\mathfrak{u}_{j}

and

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\mathfrak{b}_{j}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\sqrt{T^{0jj}-\frac{(T^{0j})^{2}}{I^{0}}}=\sqrt{\rho\theta},

which implies that
\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{j}=\chi_{j}
,
j=1,2,3
.

The proof for
\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4}=\chi_{4}
is much more complicated. It is clear that

\displaystyle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{p^{0}+\mathfrak{c}\mathfrak{e}+\mathfrak{c}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}p_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}\zeta}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(p^{0}+\mathfrak{c}\mathfrak{e})(p^{0}-\mathfrak{c}\mathfrak{e})+\mathfrak{c}(p^{0}-\mathfrak{c}\mathfrak{e})\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}p_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}\zeta(p^{0}-\mathfrak{c}\mathfrak{e})}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\operatorname{Num}}{\operatorname{Den}}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}}.

We first calculate the numerator.
Denote
\hat{A}(\gamma):=\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{6}{\gamma}-\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}
. It follows from Lemma
2.1
that

\displaystyle\hat{A}(\gamma)=-\frac{1}{\gamma}+O(\gamma^{-2}).

Now we have

\displaystyle 1+\mathfrak{e}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{\frac{1}{\gamma}-\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\gamma T_{0}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{|u|^{2}}{\gamma T_{0}}}{\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}T_{0}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}T_{0}}+\frac{1}{\gamma}-\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{|u|^{2}}{\gamma T_{0}}}{\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}T_{0}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{|u|^{2}}{T_{0}}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{\gamma u^{0}}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{\mathfrak{c}|u|^{2}}{\gamma u^{0}T_{0}}}{1-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{|u|^{2}}{T_{0}}}

and

\displaystyle\lambda_{i}=\frac{\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}T_{0}}u_{i}}{\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}T_{0}}}=\frac{\big(-\frac{1}{\gamma}+O(\gamma^{-2})\big)\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}T_{0}}u_{i}}{\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}T_{0}}},\quad i=1,2,3,

thus we obtain

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\mathfrak{e}=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\frac{1}{\gamma}-\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{|u|^{2}}{\gamma T_{0}}}{\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\hat{A}(\gamma)\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}T_{0}}}=-1,

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma(1+\mathfrak{e})=-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta},

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma\lambda_{i}=-\frac{\mathfrak{u}_{i}}{\theta},\quad i=1,2,3,

where we used the fact that

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma\Big(1-\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big)=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma\Big[-\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-1\Big)-\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)\Big]=-\frac{5}{2}-\frac{|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}.

Hence we get

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\operatorname{Num}

\displaystyle=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\Big(|p|^{2}+\gamma T_{0}(1-\mathfrak{e})(1+\mathfrak{e})+\gamma T_{0}\Big(\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\mathfrak{e}\Big)\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}p_{i}\Big)

\displaystyle=|p|^{2}-3\theta+|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathfrak{u}_{i}p_{i}=|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-3\theta.

(4.80)

We next consider the denominator. Notice that

\displaystyle\operatorname{Den}=\mathfrak{c}\zeta(p^{0}-\mathfrak{c}\mathfrak{e})=\mathfrak{c}^{2}\zeta\Big(\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\mathfrak{e}\Big)

and

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\Big(\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\mathfrak{e}\Big)=2,

then we focus on the quantity
\mathfrak{c}^{2}\zeta=T_{0}\sqrt{\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}}
. By the expression of
\zeta
in the appendix , one has

\displaystyle\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle=\Big(\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}\lambda_{j}T^{0ij}\Big)+\Big(2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}\mathfrak{e}T^{0i}+2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}}T^{00i}\Big)+\Big(\frac{T^{000}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}+\mathfrak{e}^{2}I^{0}+2\frac{\mathfrak{e}}{\mathfrak{c}}T^{00}\Big)

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{I}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{2}+\mathcal{I}_{3}.

(4.81)

It is easy to see that

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}T^{0ij}

\displaystyle=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)u^{0}u_{i}u_{j}+\mathfrak{c}^{2}K_{3}(\gamma)u^{0}\delta_{ij}\right]=\rho\mathfrak{u}_{i}\mathfrak{u}_{j}+\rho\theta\delta_{ij},

which yields that

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma^{2}\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}(\gamma\lambda_{i})\cdot(\gamma\lambda_{j})T^{0ij}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\Big(-\frac{\mathfrak{u}_{i}}{\theta}\Big)\Big(-\frac{\mathfrak{u}_{j}}{\theta}\Big)(\rho\mathfrak{u}_{i}\mathfrak{u}_{j}+\rho\theta\delta_{ij})

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho|\mathfrak{u}|^{4}}{\theta^{2}}+\frac{\rho|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{\theta}.

(4.82)

We notice that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{e}T^{0i}+\frac{T^{00i}}{\mathfrak{c}}=(\mathfrak{e}+1)T^{0i}+\Big(\frac{T^{00i}}{\mathfrak{c}}-T^{0i}\Big)

and

\displaystyle\frac{T^{00i}}{\mathfrak{c}}-T^{0i}

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(5K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}u_{i}+K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}u_{i}\right]-\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}u^{0}u_{i}

\displaystyle=n_{0}u_{i}\Big\{\frac{5}{\gamma}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{2}+\frac{|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}+\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)+\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(1-\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big)\Big\}.

(4.83)

Then it follows from (
4.79
) and (
4.1
) that

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma(\mathfrak{e}+1)T^{0i}=\rho\mathfrak{u}_{i}\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big)

and

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma\Big(\frac{T^{00i}}{\mathfrak{c}}-T^{0i}\Big)=\rho\mathfrak{u}_{i}\Big(5+0+\frac{|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}-\frac{5}{2}\Big)=\rho\mathfrak{u}_{i}\Big(\frac{5}{2}+\frac{|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big).

Hence one obtains

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma^{2}\mathcal{I}_{2}

\displaystyle=2\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{3}(\gamma\lambda_{i})\cdot\gamma\Big(\frac{T^{00i}}{\mathfrak{c}}-T^{0i}\Big)+2\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{3}(\gamma\lambda_{i})\cdot\gamma(\mathfrak{e}+1)T^{0i}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\Big(-\frac{\mathfrak{u}_{i}}{\theta}\Big)\cdot\Big[\rho\mathfrak{u}_{i}\Big(\frac{5}{2}+\frac{|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big)+\rho\mathfrak{u}_{i}\Big(-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big)\Big]

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\rho|\mathfrak{u}|^{4}}{\theta^{2}}-2\frac{\rho|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{\theta}.

(4.84)

We finally consider
\gamma^{2}\mathcal{I}_{3}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathcal{I}_{3}}{n_{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\frac{T^{000}}{n_{0}}+\mathfrak{e}^{2}\frac{I^{0}}{n_{0}}+2\frac{\mathfrak{e}}{\mathfrak{c}}\frac{T^{00}}{n_{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(3K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{3}+3K_{3}(\gamma)u^{0}|u|^{2}\right]+\mathfrak{e}^{2}\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathfrak{e}\Big(\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}(u^{0})^{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{\gamma}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{3}+\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{3}+\frac{3}{\gamma}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}+\mathfrak{e}^{2}\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}+2\mathfrak{e}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{2}-\mathfrak{e}\frac{2}{\gamma}

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{\gamma}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}+\frac{3}{\gamma}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{2}\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)+\mathfrak{e}\Big(2\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}+2\Big)\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-1\Big)\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathfrak{e}\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-1-\frac{5}{2\gamma}\Big)+\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)^{2}+2\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}(1+\mathfrak{e})\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}(1+\mathfrak{e})^{2}+\frac{3}{\gamma}\Big[\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-1\Big)\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{2}+\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}+1\Big)\Big(\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-1\Big)+(1+\mathfrak{e})\Big].

Thus we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma^{2}\mathcal{I}_{3}=\frac{3}{2}\rho+\frac{\rho|\mathfrak{u}|^{4}}{\theta^{2}}+\frac{\rho|\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{\theta}.

(4.85)

Combining (
4.1
), (
4.1
), (
4.1
) and (
4.85
), we finally obtain

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\gamma^{2}\zeta^{2}=\frac{3}{2}\rho.

Hence one obtains

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\operatorname{Den}=\theta\sqrt{6\rho},

which, together with (
4.1
), yields that

\displaystyle\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4}=\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}-3\theta}{\theta\sqrt{6\rho}}=\chi_{4}.

Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

With above preparations, we shall prove the coercivity estimate for the linear operator
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}
.

Proposition 4.13

(Uniform coercivity estimate on
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}
)
.

There exists a positive constant
\zeta_{0}>0
, which is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
, such that

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}g,g\rangle\geq\zeta_{0}\|\{\mathbf{I-P_{\mathfrak{c}}}\}g\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}

for any
g\in L_{\nu}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
.

Proof.

It is clear that one only needs to show that there is a positive constant
\zeta_{0}>0
, which is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
, such that

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}g,g\rangle\geq\zeta_{0}\|g\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}=\zeta_{0}

(4.86)

holds for any
\mathfrak{c}
and any
g\in\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\perp}
with
\|g\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}=1
.

For any given
\mathfrak{c}
, the linearized Boltzmann collision operator
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}
satisfies the well-known hypocoercivity (see
[
19
]
for instance), i.e., there exists a positive constant
\alpha_{\mathfrak{c}}>0
, such that

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}g,g\rangle\geq\alpha_{\mathfrak{c}}\|g\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}=\alpha_{\mathfrak{c}}

(4.87)

for any
g\in\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\perp}
with
\|g\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}=1
. Denote

\displaystyle\zeta_{\mathfrak{c}}:=\inf_{\begin{subarray}{c}g\in\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\perp}\\
\|g\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}=1\end{subarray}}\langle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}g,g\rangle.

(4.88)

It follows from (
4.87
) that
\zeta_{\mathfrak{c}}\geq\alpha_{\mathfrak{c}}>0
for any
\mathfrak{c}
. To prove (
4.86
), it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\inf_{\mathfrak{c}\geq 1}\zeta_{\mathfrak{c}}>0.

(4.89)

We prove (
4.89
) by contradiction. Assume that (
4.89
) is not true, then there exists a sequence
\{\zeta_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}\}
such that

\displaystyle\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\mathfrak{c}_{n}=\infty\quad\text{and}\quad\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\zeta_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}=0.

(4.90)

For each
n
, owing to (
4.88
), there exists
g_{n}\in\mathcal{N}^{\perp}_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}
with
\|g_{n}\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}}=1
, so that

\displaystyle\zeta_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}\leq\langle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}g_{n},g_{n}\rangle<\zeta_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}+\frac{1}{n},

which, together with (
4.90
), yields that

\displaystyle\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\langle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}g_{n},g_{n}\rangle=0.

(4.91)

It is clear that
\{g_{n}\}^{\infty}_{n=1}
is a bounded sequence in
L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
. Since
L^{2}
is a Hilbert space, based on the Eberlein-Smulian theorem, we have the weakly convergent sequence (up to extracting a subsequence with an abuse of notation)
g_{n}\rightharpoonup g
in
L^{2}
. Moreover, for any fixed
N\geq 1
, one has

\displaystyle\chi_{\{|p|\leq N\}}\sqrt{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}}g_{n}\rightharpoonup\chi_{\{|p|\leq N\}}\sqrt{\nu}g\quad\text{in}\ L^{2},

where
\nu(p)=\lim_{\mathfrak{c}\rightarrow\infty}\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)
. Hence, by the weak semi-continuity, for any fixed
N
, we have

\displaystyle\|\chi_{\{|p|\leq N\}}\sqrt{\nu}g\|_{2}\leq\liminf\limits_{n\rightarrow\infty}\|\chi_{\{|p|\leq N\}}\sqrt{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}}g_{n}\|_{2}\leq 1,

which implies that

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{\nu}g\|_{2}\leq 1.

(4.92)

For later use, we denote

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}f:=\nu f-\mathbf{K}f,

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}f:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}k(p,q)f(q)dq=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[k_{2}(p,q)-k_{1}(p,q)]f(q)dq

(4.93)

with
k_{1}(p,q)
and
k_{2}(p,q)
defined in (
4.42
)–(
4.43
). We also denote
\mathcal{N}
as the null space of
\mathbf{L}
, that is,
\mathcal{N}:=\operatorname{span}\{\chi_{0},\chi_{1},\chi_{2},\chi_{3},\chi_{4}\}
.
Clearly, we have

\displaystyle 0\leq\left\langle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}g_{n},g_{n}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\|g_{n}\right\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}}^{2}-\left\langle(\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}-\mathbf{K})g_{n},g_{n}\right\rangle-\left\langle\mathbf{K}g_{n},g_{n}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=1-\left\langle(\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}-\mathbf{K})g_{n},g_{n}\right\rangle-\left\langle\mathbf{K}g_{n},g_{n}\right\rangle.

(4.94)

Since
\mathbf{K}
is a compact operator on
L^{2}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\|\mathbf{K}g_{n}-\mathbf{K}g\|_{2}=0.

Hence we have

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{K}g_{n},g_{n}\rangle-\langle\mathbf{K}g,g\rangle=\langle\mathbf{K}g_{n}-\mathbf{K}g,g_{n}\rangle+\langle\mathbf{K}g,g_{n}-g\rangle\rightarrow 0,\quad n\rightarrow\infty.

It follows from Lemmas
4.8
–
4.9
that

\displaystyle\left\langle(\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}-\mathbf{K})g_{n},g_{n}\right\rangle\rightarrow 0,\quad n\rightarrow\infty.

(4.95)

Combining (
4.91
), (
4.1
)-(
4.95
), we have

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{K}g,g\rangle=1,

(4.96)

which, together with (
4.92
), yields that

\displaystyle 0\leq\langle\mathbf{L}g,g\rangle=\|g\|^{2}_{\nu}-\langle\mathbf{K}g,g\rangle\leq 0.

Thus we have
g\in\mathcal{N}
.

Next, we shall show that
g\in\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
. Recall
\chi^{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}_{\alpha}
,
\chi_{\alpha}
defined in (
4.77
) (with
\mathfrak{c}
replaced by
\mathfrak{c}_{n}
) and (
4.78
). Notice that

\displaystyle 0=\langle g_{n},\chi^{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}_{\alpha}\rangle=\langle g_{n}-g,\chi^{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}_{\alpha}-\chi_{\alpha}\rangle+\langle g_{n}-g,\chi_{\alpha}\rangle+\langle g,\chi^{\mathfrak{c}_{n}}_{\alpha}-\chi_{\alpha}\rangle+\langle g,\chi_{\alpha}\rangle,\quad\alpha=0,1,\cdots,4.

(4.97)

Using Lemma
4.12
and
g_{n}\rightharpoonup g
in
L^{2}
, we take the limit
n\rightarrow\infty
in (
4.97
) to obtain

\displaystyle\langle g,\chi_{\alpha}\rangle=0,\quad\alpha=0,1,\cdots,4,

which implies that
g\in\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
. Since we also have
g\in\mathcal{N}
, one concludes that
g=0
, which contradicts with (
4.96
).
Therefore the proof of Proposition
4.13
is completed.
∎

4.2.
Uniform estimate on
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-1}

To apply the Hilbert expansion procedure, we need uniform-in-
\mathfrak{c}
estimate on
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-1}
. The proof is inspired by
[
33
]
.

Lemma 4.14
.

For any fixed
0\leq\lambda<1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}f(p)|\lesssim\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{2},\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3}.

Proof.

It follows from (
2.2
) and Lemma
4.3
that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}1}f(p)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1-\lambda}{2}}(p)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1}{2}}(q)|f(q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|e^{-(1-\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|p|}e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|q|}|f(q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{1}}{2}|q|}|f(q)|dq\lesssim\|f\|_{2}.

Using (
2.3
), one has

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}2}f(p)|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{p^{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dq}{q^{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dq^{\prime}}{q^{\prime 0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dp^{\prime}}{p^{\prime 0}}W\left(p,q\mid p^{\prime},q^{\prime}\right)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1+\lambda}{2}}(q)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1-\lambda}{2}}(q^{\prime})|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p^{\prime})f(p^{\prime})|

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{p^{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dq}{q^{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dq^{\prime}}{q^{\prime 0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dp^{\prime}}{p^{\prime 0}}\bar{s}\delta^{(4)}\left(p^{\mu}+p^{\prime\mu}-q^{\mu}-q^{\prime\mu}\right)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1+\lambda}{2}}(p^{\prime})\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1-\lambda}{2}}(q^{\prime})|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(q)f(q)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\xi(p,q)|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(q)f(q)|dq,

(4.98)

where we exchanged
p^{\prime}
and
q
in the last second step with

\displaystyle\xi(p,q):=\frac{\mathfrak{c}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dq^{\prime}}{q^{\prime 0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dp^{\prime}}{p^{\prime 0}}\bar{s}\delta^{(4)}\left(p^{\mu}+p^{\prime\mu}-q^{\mu}-q^{\prime\mu}\right)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1-\lambda}{2}}(p^{\prime})\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1-\lambda}{2}}(q^{\prime})

and

\bar{g}^{2}=g^{2}+\frac{1}{2}(p^{\mu}+q^{\mu})\cdot\left(p^{\prime\mu}+q^{\prime\mu}-p^{\mu}-q^{\mu}\right),\quad\bar{s}=\bar{g}^{2}+4\mathfrak{c}^{2}.

Applying Lorentz transformation for
\xi(p,q)
, one has

\displaystyle\xi(p,q)=\frac{\mathfrak{c}c_{0}^{1-\lambda}}{p^{0}q^{0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dq^{\prime}}{q^{\prime 0}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{dp^{\prime}}{p^{\prime 0}}s(\bar{p},p^{\prime})\delta^{(4)}\left(\bar{p}^{\mu}+p^{\prime\mu}-\bar{q}^{\mu}-q^{\prime\mu}\right)e^{-(1-\lambda)\frac{\mathfrak{c}(p^{\prime 0}+q^{\prime 0})}{2T_{0}}},

where
s(\bar{p},p^{\prime})=-(\bar{p}^{\mu}+p^{\prime\mu})(\bar{p}_{\mu}+p^{\prime}_{\mu})
.
By similar arguments as in
[
47
]
, one can show that

\displaystyle\xi(p,q)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathfrak{c}c_{0}^{1-\lambda}\pi s^{3/2}}{4gp^{0}q^{0}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{y\left(1+\sqrt{y^{2}+1}\right)}{\sqrt{y^{2}+1}}e^{-\frac{1-\lambda}{2T_{0}}\mathfrak{c}(\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0})\sqrt{y^{2}+1}}I_{0}\left(\frac{(1-\lambda)\mathfrak{c}|\bar{p}\times\bar{q}|}{gT_{0}}y\right)dy
