\displaystyle=\frac{\mathfrak{c}c_{0}^{1-\lambda}\pi s^{3/2}}{4gp^{0}q^{0}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{y\left(1+\sqrt{y^{2}+1}\right)}{\sqrt{y^{2}+1}}e^{-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}\sqrt{y^{2}+1}}I_{0}\left(\tilde{\boldsymbol{j}}y\right)dy,

(4.99)

where

\displaystyle c_{0}=\frac{n_{0}}{4\pi\mathfrak{c}T_{0}K_{2}(\gamma)},\quad\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}}=(1-\lambda)\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}},\quad\tilde{\boldsymbol{j}}=(1-\lambda)\bar{\boldsymbol{j}},\quad\bar{\boldsymbol{\ell}}=\mathfrak{c}\frac{\bar{p}^{0}+\bar{q}^{0}}{2T_{0}},\quad\bar{\boldsymbol{j}}=\mathfrak{c}\frac{|\bar{p}\times\bar{q}|}{gT_{0}}.

In view of (
2.6
)–(
2.8
), we can rewrite (
4.2
) as

\displaystyle\xi(p,q)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathfrak{c}c_{0}^{1-\lambda}\pi s^{3/2}}{4gp^{0}q^{0}}[J_{1}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}},\tilde{\boldsymbol{j}})+J_{2}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\ell}},\tilde{\boldsymbol{j}})].

By similar arguments as in Lemma
4.3
, one can prove

\displaystyle\xi(p,q)\lesssim\Big[\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{1}{|p-q|}\Big]e^{-(1-\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|p-q|},

(4.100)

which yields that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\xi^{2}(p,q)dq\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}+\frac{1}{|p-q|^{2}}\Big)e^{-2(1-\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|p-q|}dq<C<\infty,

(4.101)

where
C
is a positive constant independent of
\mathfrak{c}
. Hence it follows from (
4.2
) and (
4.101
) that

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}2}f(p)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\xi^{2}(p,q)dq\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(q)f(q)|^{2}dq\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{2}.

Therefore the proof of Lemma
4.14
is completed.
∎

Lemma 4.15
.

For any fixed
0\leq\lambda<1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}1}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle\right|+\left|\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}2}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle\right|\lesssim\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{2}^{2}.

Proof.

It follows from (
2.2
) and Lemma
4.3
that

\displaystyle\left|\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}1}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1-\lambda}{2}}(p)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1+\lambda}{2}}(q)\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)f(p)|\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(q)f(q)|dpdq

\displaystyle\lesssim\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|e^{-(1-\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|p|}e^{-(1+\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|q|}\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)f(p)|\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(q)f(q)|dpdq

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big(\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|e^{-(1-\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|p|}e^{-(1+\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|q|}\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)f(p)|^{2}dpdq\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big(\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|p-q|e^{-(1-\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|p|}e^{-(1+\lambda)\bar{c}_{1}|q|}\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(q)f(q)|^{2}dpdq\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{2}^{2}.

Using (
4.2
) and (
4.100
), one has

\displaystyle\left|\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}2}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\xi(p,q)|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(q)f(q)|\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)f(p)|dpdq

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big(\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\xi(p,q)\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)f(p)|^{2}dpdq\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big(\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\xi(p,q)\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(q)f(q)|^{2}dpdq\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{2}^{2}.

Therefore the proof of Lemma
4.15
is completed.
∎

Lemma 4.16
.

For any fixed
0\leq\lambda<1
, there exists a positive constant
C
which is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
, such that

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}-C\|f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}.

Proof.

For any
r>0
, it follows from Lemmas
4.15
and
4.6
that

\displaystyle\left|\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big\{\int_{|p|\leq r}+\int_{|p|\geq r}\Big\}|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)f(p)|^{2}dp

\displaystyle\lesssim\max\Big\{\frac{1}{1+r},\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big\}\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}+C_{r}\|f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}.

Noting
\mathfrak{c}\gg 1
, taking
r
suitably large, we have

\displaystyle\left|\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{K}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle\right|\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}+C\|f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2},

which, together with
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f=\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}f-\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}f
, yields that

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle-\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}-C\|f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}.

Therefore the proof of Lemma
4.16
is completed.
∎

Proposition 4.17
.

For any fixed
0\leq\lambda<1
,
m>\frac{3}{2}
, suppose
g\in\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\perp}
and

\displaystyle\|(1+|p|)^{m}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}g\|_{\infty}<\infty,

then it holds

\displaystyle|\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-1}g(p)|\lesssim\|(1+|p|)^{m}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}g\|_{\infty}\cdot\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p),\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3},

(4.102)

where the constant is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
.

Proof.

Let
f=\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-1}g\in\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\perp}
, then we have
g=\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f=\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}f-\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}f
. Using Lemma
4.14
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|f(p)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-1}(p)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|g(p)|+\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-1}(p)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}f(p)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|g(p)|+\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|\mathbf{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}f(p)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|g(p)|+\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}.

(4.103)

By Proposition
4.13
and Lemma
4.16
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle+\|f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\langle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\rangle+\langle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f,f\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+|p|)^{m}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|g(p)|\cdot(1+|p|)^{-m}\cdot|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)f(p)|dp

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}\cdot\|(1+|p|)^{m}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}g\|_{\infty}\cdot\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{(1+|p|)^{2m}}dp\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}},

which, together with
m>\frac{3}{2}
, yields that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}f\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}\lesssim\|(1+|p|)^{m}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}g\|_{\infty}<\infty.

(4.104)

Combining (
4.104
) and (
4.2
), one has

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}(p)|f(p)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(1+|p|)^{m}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{\lambda}{2}}g\|_{\infty},

which concludes (
4.102
). Therefore the proof of Proposition
4.17
is completed.
∎

5.
Uniform-in-
\mathfrak{c}
estimates on the linear part of Hilbert expansion

5.1.
Reformulation of
F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}

For
n=0,1,\cdots,2k-2
, we decompose
F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}
as

\displaystyle\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}=\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)+\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\Big(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big),

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)=\Big[a_{n+1}+b_{n+1}\cdot p+c_{n+1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big]\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}.

(5.1)

Using (
1.13
)–(
1.14
) and Lemma
4.11
, by tedious calculations, one has

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big[a_{n+1}+b_{n+1}\cdot p+c_{n+1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big]\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}a_{n+1}+\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}u^{0}(u\cdot b_{n+1})+\frac{e_{0}(u^{0})^{2}+P_{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{4}}c_{n+1},

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{p_{j}p}{p^{0}}F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{p_{j}p}{p^{0}}\left[a_{n+1}+b_{n+1}\cdot p+c_{n+1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\right]\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{p_{j}p}{p^{0}}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\left(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\right)dp

\displaystyle=\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}u_{j}ua_{n+1}+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)u_{j}u\left[\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}c_{n+1}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbf{e}_{j}a_{n+1}\frac{P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}n_{0}K_{3}(\gamma)}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left(ub_{n+1,j}+u_{j}b_{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbf{e}_{j}\frac{\mathfrak{c}n_{0}K_{3}(\gamma)}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}c_{n+1}\right]+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{p_{j}p}{p^{0}}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\left(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\right)dp,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{p}_{j}F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{p}_{j}\Big[a_{n+1}+b_{n+1}\cdot p+c_{n+1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big]\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{p}_{j}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\left(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\right)dp

\displaystyle=n_{0}u_{j}a_{n+1}+\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}u_{j}\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+P_{0}b_{n+1,j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}u^{0}u_{j}c_{n+1}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{p}_{j}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\left(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\right)dp,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}p_{j}F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}p_{j}\Big[a_{n+1}+b_{n+1}\cdot p+c_{n+1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big]\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)u^{0}u_{j}\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+\mathfrak{c}^{2}K_{3}(\gamma)u^{0}b_{n+1,j}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(5K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}\right]u_{j}c_{n+1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}u^{0}u_{j}a_{n+1},

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}p^{0}F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}p^{0}\left[a_{n+1}+b_{n+1}\cdot p+c_{n+1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\right]\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(5K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}\right]\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(3K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+3K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}\right]u^{0}c_{n+1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{e_{0}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+P_{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}a_{n+1},

where
\mathbf{e}_{j}\ (j=1,2,3)
are the unit base vectors in
\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

Next, we shall derive the equation for
(a_{n+1},b_{n+1},c_{n+1})
. Notice that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j=n+2\\
i,j\geq 0\end{subarray}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})\ (\text{or}\ \sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j=n+2\\
i,j\geq 1\end{subarray}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})\ \text{when $n=2k-2$}).

(5.2)

Integrating (
5.2
) with respect to
p
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}a_{n+1}+\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}u^{0}\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+\frac{e_{0}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+P_{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{4}}c_{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\nabla_{x}\cdot\left(n_{0}ua_{n+1}+\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}u\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+P_{0}b_{n+1}+\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}u^{0}uc_{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\nabla_{x}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{p}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\left(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\right)dp=0.

(5.3)

Multiplying (
5.2
) by
p_{j}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, one gets

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}u^{0}u_{j}a_{n+1}+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)u^{0}u_{j}\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+\mathfrak{c}^{2}K_{3}(\gamma)u^{0}b_{n+1,j}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(5K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}\right]u_{j}c_{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\nabla_{x}\cdot\left(\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}u_{j}ua_{n+1}+\frac{n_{0}}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)u_{j}u\left[\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}c_{n+1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\partial_{x_{j}}\left(P_{0}a_{n+1}\right)+\nabla_{x}\cdot\left[\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}n_{0}K_{3}(\gamma)}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left(ub_{n+1,j}+u_{j}b_{n+1}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\partial_{x_{j}}\left(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}n_{0}K_{3}(\gamma)}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}c_{n+1}\right]\right)+\nabla_{x}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}p_{j}\hat{p}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\left(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\right)dp=0

(5.4)

for
j=1,2,3
with
b_{n+1}=\left(b_{n+1,1},b_{n+1,2},b_{n+1,3}\right)^{t}
.

Multiplying (
5.2
) by
\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, one obtains that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\frac{e_{0}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+P_{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{4}}a_{n+1}+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(5K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}\right]\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(3K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+3K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}\right]u^{0}c_{n+1}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\nabla_{x}\cdot\left(\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}u^{0}ua_{n+1}+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)u^{0}u\left(u\cdot b_{n+1}\right)+\mathfrak{c}^{2}K_{3}(\gamma)u^{0}b_{n+1}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left[\left(5K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right)\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+K_{3}(\gamma)|u|^{2}\right]uc_{n+1}\right)=0.

(5.5)

After a tedious computation, we can rewrite (
5.1
)–(
5.1
) into the following linear symmetric hyperbolic system:

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{0}\partial_{t}U_{n+1}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\mathbf{A}_{i}\partial_{i}U_{n+1}+\mathbf{B}U_{n+1}=\mathbf{S}_{n+1},

(5.6)

where

U_{n+1}=\begin{pmatrix}a_{n+1}\\
b_{n+1}\\
c_{n+1}\end{pmatrix},\quad\mathbf{S}_{n+1}=\begin{pmatrix}-\nabla_{x}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{p}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\Big(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)dp\\
-\nabla_{x}\cdot\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}p\otimes\hat{p}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\Big(\frac{F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)dp\\
0\end{pmatrix}.

The matrices
\mathbf{A}_{0},\mathbf{A}_{i}\ (i=1,2,3)
and
\mathbf{B}
depend only on the smooth relativistic Euler solution
(n_{0},u,T_{0})
. To express these matrices, we denote

h(t,x):=\frac{e_{0}+P_{0}}{n_{0}},\quad h_{1}(t,x):=\frac{n_{0}}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}\left(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)\right),\quad h_{2}(t,x):=\frac{n_{0}K_{3}(\gamma)}{\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}.

Then the matrices
\mathbf{A}_{0},\mathbf{A}_{i},(i=1,2,3)
in (
5.6
) are

\mathbf{A}_{0}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}&\frac{n_{0}u^{0}hu^{t}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}&\frac{e_{0}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+P_{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{4}}\\
\frac{n_{0}u^{0}hu^{t}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}&\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\mathfrak{c}}u\otimes u+\mathfrak{c}h_{2}\mathbf{I}\right)u^{0}&\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}-h_{2}\right)u\\
\frac{e_{0}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}+P_{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{4}}&\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}-h_{2}\right)u^{t}&\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}-\frac{3h_{2}}{\mathfrak{c}}\right)u^{0}\end{array}\right)

and

\mathbf{A}_{i}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}n_{0}u_{i}&\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}n_{0}hu_{i}u^{t}+P_{0}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{t}&\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}n_{0}hu^{0}u_{i}\\
\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}n_{0}hu_{i}u+P_{0}\mathbf{e}_{i}&h_{1}u_{i}u\otimes u+\mathfrak{c}^{2}h_{2}\left(u_{i}\mathbf{I}+\tilde{\mathbf{A}}_{i}\right)&\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\mathfrak{c}}u_{i}u+\mathfrak{c}h_{2}\mathbf{e}_{i}\right)u^{0}\\
\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}n_{0}hu^{0}u_{i}&\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\mathfrak{c}}u_{i}u^{t}+\mathfrak{c}h_{2}\mathbf{e}_{i}^{t}\right)u^{0}&\left(\frac{h_{1}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\left(u^{0}\right)^{2}-h_{2}\right)u_{i}\end{array}\right),

where

\displaystyle\big(\tilde{\mathbf{A}_{i}}\big)_{jk}=\delta_{ij}u_{k}+\delta_{ik}u_{j},\quad 1\leq j,k\leq 3.

The matrix
\mathbf{B}=(b_{ij})
has the form

\displaystyle b_{11}=0,\quad(b_{12},b_{13},b_{14})=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{hu^{t}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big)+n_{0}u^{t}\Big[\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{hu}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big)\Big]^{t}+(\nabla_{x}P_{0})^{t},

\displaystyle b_{15}=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{hu^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big)+\frac{n_{0}u}{\mathfrak{c}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{hu^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big)-\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big),

\displaystyle(b_{21},b_{31},b_{41})=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{hu}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big)+\nabla_{x}P_{0}+\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{hu}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big)n_{0}u,

\displaystyle(b_{j2},b_{j3},b_{j4})=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\partial_{t}\Big[\frac{h_{1}}{n_{0}}u_{j}u^{t}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}h_{2}}{n_{0}}\mathbf{e}_{j}^{t}\Big]+n_{0}(u\cdot\nabla_{x})\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{n_{0}}u_{j}u^{t}\Big)+n_{0}u^{t}\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}h_{2}}{n_{0}}\Big)\mathbf{e}_{j}^{t}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\Big[\nabla_{x}(\mathfrak{c}^{2}h_{2}u_{j})\Big]^{t}+\partial_{x_{j}}(\mathfrak{c}^{2}h_{2}u^{t}),

\displaystyle b_{j5}=-\partial_{t}(h_{2}u_{j})+\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{n_{0}}u_{j}u^{0}\Big)+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}u^{t}\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{n_{0}}u_{j}u^{0}\Big)+\partial_{x_{j}}(\mathfrak{c}h_{2}u^{0}),

\displaystyle b_{51}=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{hu^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big)+\frac{n_{0}u}{\mathfrak{c}}\cdot\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{hu^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big)-\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{P_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\Big),

\displaystyle(b_{52},b_{53},b_{54})=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{n_{0}}u^{0}u^{t}\Big)-\partial_{t}(h_{2}u^{t})+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}u^{t}\Big[\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{n_{0}}u^{0}u\Big)\Big]^{t}+\Big(\nabla(\mathfrak{c}h_{2}u^{0})\Big)^{t},

\displaystyle b_{55}=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{3}}\partial_{t}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{n_{0}}(u^{0})^{2}-3\mathfrak{c}^{2}\frac{h_{2}}{n_{0}}|u|^{2}\Big)+\frac{n_{0}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}u\cdot\nabla_{x}\Big(\frac{h_{1}}{n_{0}}(u^{0})^{2}-3\mathfrak{c}^{2}\frac{h_{2}}{n_{0}}|u|^{2}\Big)+\nabla_{x}\cdot(2h_{2}u).

Next, we prove the positivity of
\mathbf{A}_{0}
. Set
\phi(\gamma):=\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}
. A direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle\det(\mathbf{A}_{0})_{1\times 1}\geq\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}>0,

\displaystyle\quad\det(\mathbf{A}_{0})_{2\times 2}\geq\Big(\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{2}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\phi}{\gamma}>0,

\displaystyle\det(\mathbf{A}_{0})_{3\times 3}\geq\Big(\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{3}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\phi}{\gamma}\Big)^{2}>0,

\displaystyle\quad\det(\mathbf{A}_{0})_{4\times 4}\geq\Big(\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{4}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\phi}{\gamma}\Big)^{3}>0,

and

\displaystyle\det\mathbf{A}_{0}=\Big(\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{5}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\phi}{\gamma}\Big)^{3}(u^{0})^{-2}\Big\{|u|^{2}\mathfrak{c}^{2}(\Psi-\frac{\Psi}{\gamma\phi}-\frac{\phi}{\gamma})+\mathfrak{c}^{4}(\Psi-\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}-\frac{\phi}{\gamma})\Big\},

(5.7)

where
\Psi:=1+\frac{6}{\gamma}\phi-\phi^{2}
. To prove the positivity of (
5.7
), we use
[
44
, Proposition 10]
to get

\displaystyle\phi^{2}-\frac{5}{\gamma}\phi+\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}-1<0,

(5.8)

which yields that

\displaystyle\Psi-\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}-\frac{\phi}{\gamma}=1+\frac{6}{\gamma}\phi-\phi^{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}-\frac{\phi}{\gamma}=-(\phi^{2}-\frac{5}{\gamma}\phi+\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}-1)>0.

(5.9)

A direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{h}:=

\displaystyle\Psi-\frac{\Psi}{\gamma\phi}-\frac{\phi}{\gamma}=\frac{1}{\phi\Psi}(\phi\Psi-\frac{\Psi}{\gamma}-\frac{\phi^{2}}{\gamma})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\phi\Psi}\Big\{\phi(1+\frac{6}{\gamma}\phi-\phi^{2})-\frac{1}{\gamma}(1+\frac{6}{\gamma}\phi-\phi^{2})-\frac{\phi^{2}}{\gamma}\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\phi\Psi}\Big\{\phi^{3}+\frac{1}{\gamma}+\frac{6}{\gamma^{2}}\phi-\phi-\frac{6}{\gamma}\phi^{2}\Big\}.

Observing
\phi=\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}=\frac{4}{\gamma}+\frac{K_{1}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}:=\frac{4}{\gamma}+\phi_{1}
, one has

\displaystyle\phi^{3}+\frac{1}{\gamma}+\frac{6}{\gamma^{2}}\phi-\phi-\frac{6}{\gamma}\phi^{2}

\displaystyle=\gamma\phi_{1}^{3}+6\phi_{1}^{2}+\frac{6}{\gamma}\phi_{1}-\gamma\phi_{1}-3-\frac{8}{\gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle=(\gamma\phi_{1}^{3}+4\phi_{1}^{2}-\gamma\phi_{1}-1)+\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}(\gamma^{2}\phi_{1}^{2}+3\gamma\phi_{1}-\gamma^{2}-3)-\frac{2}{\gamma^{2}}.

(5.10)

Applying
[
44
, Proposion 10]
again, we know that both of the first two terms on the RHS of (
5.1
) are negative. Thus one gets

\displaystyle\mathfrak{h}>0,

which, together with (
5.7
) and (
5.9
), yields that

\displaystyle\det\mathbf{A}_{0}\geq\Big(\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)^{5}\Big(\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}\phi}{\gamma}\Big)^{3}\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{4}}{(u^{0})^{2}}\{-(\phi^{2}-\frac{5}{\gamma}\phi+\frac{1}{\gamma^{2}}-1)\}>0.

Therefore
\mathbf{A}_{0}
is actually a positive definite matrix.

5.2.
Uniform-in-
\mathfrak{c}
estimates on
F^{\mathfrak{c}}_{n}

Proposition 5.1
.

Let the local relativistic Maxwellian
F^{\mathfrak{c}}_{0}=\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(n_{0},u,T_{0};p)
be as in (
1.12
) formed by
(n_{0}(t,x),u(t,x),T_{0}(t,x))
which is a smooth solution to the relativistic Euler equations (
3.1
) on a time interval
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Then we can construct the smooth terms
F^{\mathfrak{c}}_{1},\ldots,F^{\mathfrak{c}}_{2k-1}
of the Hilbert expansion in
(t,x)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
such that, for any
0<\lambda<1
, the following estimates hold

\displaystyle\left|F^{\mathfrak{c}}_{n}(t,x,p)\right|\leq C(\lambda)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\lambda}(n_{0}(t,x),u(t,x),T_{0}(t,x);p),\quad n=1,2,\ldots,2k-1

(5.11)

and

\displaystyle\left|\partial^{m}F^{\mathfrak{c}}_{n}(t,x,p)\right|\leq C(\lambda)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\lambda}(n_{0}(t,x),u(t,x),T_{0}(t,x);p),\quad n=1,2,\ldots,2k-1,\quad m\geq 1,

(5.12)

where
\partial^{m}:=\partial^{m}_{t,x}
. We emphasize that the constants in (
5.11
) and (
5.12
) are independent of
\mathfrak{c}
.

Proof.

It is noted that
\mathbf{A}_{0}
,
\mathbf{A}_{i}
and
\mathbf{B}
in (
5.6
) depend only on the smooth functions
n_{0}(t,x)
,
u(t,x)
and
T_{0}(t,x)
.
Denote
\psi_{1}:=\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\Big(\frac{F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)
, then one has

\displaystyle F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}=\Big(a_{1}+b_{1}\cdot p+c_{1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}+\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1},

which yields that

\displaystyle\partial F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}=\Big(\partial a_{1}+\partial b_{1}\cdot p+\partial c_{1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}+\Big(a_{1}+b_{1}\cdot p+c_{1}\frac{p^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\Big)\partial\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}+\partial\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1}+\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\partial\psi_{1},

(5.13)

where
\partial=\partial_{t}
or
\partial=\partial_{x_{j}}
for
j=1,2,3
. A direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle|\partial\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}|\leq C\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{1-},

where
C
depends on
\|\nabla_{t,x}(n_{0},u,T_{0})\|_{\infty}
. We denote

\displaystyle g_{1}:=Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1},\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}),

then it follows from (
1.10
) that

\displaystyle g_{1}=\partial_{t}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},

(5.14)

which implies that
|\partial^{m}g_{1}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{1-}
for any
m\geq 0
.

To estimate
\partial\psi_{1}
, we apply
\partial
to (
5.14
) to obtain

\displaystyle\partial g_{1}

\displaystyle=Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\partial\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1},\partial\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\partial\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\partial\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1},\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})

\displaystyle\qquad+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\partial\psi_{1})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\partial\psi_{1},\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}),

which yields that

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}(\{\mathbf{I-P_{\mathfrak{c}}}\}\partial\psi_{1})=\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}\partial\psi_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\partial g_{1}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\partial\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1},\partial\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})\Big\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\partial\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\partial\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}\psi_{1},\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})\Big\}.

(5.15)

Using the exponential decay of
\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-1}
in Proposition
4.17
, we have

\displaystyle|\psi_{1}|=\Big|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}\Big(\frac{F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)\Big|=\Big|\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-1}\Big(-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}(\partial_{t}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})\Big)\Big|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1}{2}-},

(5.16)

which, together with
|\partial g_{1}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{1-}
, yields that the RHS of (
5.2
) can be bounded by
\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1}{2}-}
. Using Proposition
4.17
again, we obtain

\displaystyle|\{\mathbf{I-P_{\mathfrak{c}}}\}\partial\psi_{1}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1}{2}-}.

(5.17)

On the other hand, it is clear that

\displaystyle|\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\partial\psi_{1}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1}{2}-},

which, together with (
5.17
), implies that

\displaystyle|\partial\psi_{1}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1}{2}-}.

Similarly, one can deduce that

\displaystyle|\partial^{m}\psi_{1}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1}{2}-},\quad m\geq 1.

(5.18)

Next we consider the estimate on the macroscopic parts
(a_{1},b_{1},c_{1})
. Using (
5.18
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{S}_{1}\|_{H^{N_{0}-1}}\lesssim 1.

One obtains from Lemma
3.1
that

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{A}_{0}\right\|_{\infty}+\sum_{\alpha=0}^{3}\left\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{A}_{\alpha}\right\|_{H^{N_{0}-1}}+\left\|\mathbf{B}\right\|_{H^{N_{0}-1}}\lesssim 1.

Applying standard energy estimate, one gets

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left(a_{1},b_{1},c_{1}\right)(t)\right\|_{H^{N_{0}-3}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\left(a_{1},b_{1},c_{1}\right)(t)\right\|_{H^{N_{0}-3}}^{2}+\left\|\left(a_{1},b_{1},c_{1}\right)(t)\right\|_{H^{N_{0}-3}},

which, together with Grönwall’s inequality, yields that

\displaystyle\left\|\left(a_{1},b_{1},c_{1}\right)(t)\right\|_{H^{N_{0}-3}}\lesssim 1.

(5.19)

Hence it follows from (
5.1
) that

\displaystyle\Big|\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)\Big|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{\frac{1}{2}-},

which, together with (
5.16
), yields that

\displaystyle|F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{1-}.

For
\partial F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}
, on account of (
5.13
), (
5.18
) and (
5.19
), one obtains

\displaystyle|\partial F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{1-}.

Similar arguments lead to

\displaystyle|\partial^{m}F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{1-},\quad m\geq 1.

By induction, we can prove that

\displaystyle|F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{1-},\quad|\partial^{m}F_{n+1}^{\mathfrak{c}}|\lesssim\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}^{1-},\quad n=0,1,\cdots,2k-2,\quad m\geq 1.

Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

6.
Uniform in
\mathfrak{c}
and
\varepsilon
estimates on the remainder
F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}_{R}

In this section, we shall prove our main results, Theorem
1.1
and Theorem
1.5
.
As in
[
25
,
46
]
, we define

\displaystyle f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)=\frac{F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}_{R}(t,x,p)}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)}}

(6.1)

and

\displaystyle h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)=\frac{F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}_{R}(t,x,p)}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)}}.

(6.2)

We first present two uniform-in-
\mathfrak{c}
estimates on the nonlinear operators.

Lemma 6.1
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{w_{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(h_{1}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}},h_{2}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}})\Big|\lesssim\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)\|h_{1}\|_{\infty,\ell}\|h_{2}\|_{\infty,\ell},

where the constant is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
.

Proof.

Noting

\displaystyle p^{0}+q^{0}=p^{\prime 0}+q^{\prime 0},\quad p+q=p^{\prime}+q^{\prime},

we claim that

\displaystyle|p|\lesssim|p^{\prime}|+|q^{\prime}|,\quad|q|\lesssim|p^{\prime}|+|q^{\prime}|.

(6.3)

Actually, without loss of generality, we may assume that
|p|\leq|q|
. Denote
r:=\max\{|p^{\prime}|,|q^{\prime}|\}
, then one has

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|p|^{2}}\leq\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|p|^{2}}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|q|^{2}}=\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|p^{\prime}|^{2}}+\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+|q^{\prime}|^{2}}\leq 2\sqrt{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+r^{2}},

which yields that

\displaystyle|p|^{2}\leq r^{2}\leq|p^{\prime}|^{2}+|q^{\prime}|^{2},

Thus it holds

\displaystyle|p|\leq|p^{\prime}|+|q^{\prime}|.

(6.4)

If
|p|\leq\frac{|q|}{2}
, one has
|p+q|\geq|q|-|p|\geq\frac{|q|}{2}
, which yields that

\displaystyle\frac{|q|}{2}\leq|p+q|=|p^{\prime}+q^{\prime}|\leq|p^{\prime}|+|q^{\prime}|.

If
\frac{|q|}{2}\leq|p|\leq|q|
, it follows from (
6.4
) that

\displaystyle|q|\leq 2|p|\leq 2(|p^{\prime}|+|q^{\prime}|).

Hence the claim (
6.3
) holds.

Now it follows from (
6.3
) that

\displaystyle w_{\ell}(p)\lesssim w_{\ell}(p^{\prime})w_{\ell}(q^{\prime}),

which, together with from (
4.41
), yields that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{w_{\ell}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(h_{1}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}},h_{2}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}})\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{w_{\ell}(p)}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\Big|h_{1}(p^{\prime})h_{2}(q^{\prime})\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(p^{\prime})\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q^{\prime})}-h_{1}(p)h_{2}(q)\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)}\Big|d\omega dq

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\Big[|w_{\ell}(p^{\prime})h_{1}(p^{\prime})|\cdot|w_{\ell}(q^{\prime})h_{2}(q^{\prime})|+|w_{\ell}(p)h_{1}(p)|\cdot|h(q)|\Big]\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)}d\omega dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)\|h_{1}\|_{\infty,\ell}\|h_{2}\|_{\infty,\ell}.

Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

Lemma 6.2
.

For any
\ell\geq 9
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\left(h_{1},h_{2}\right),h_{3}\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\left\|h_{3}\right\|_{\infty,\ell}\left\|h_{2}\right\|_{2}\left\|h_{1}\right\|_{2}.

Furthermore, if
\chi(p)
satisfies
|\chi(p)|\lesssim e^{-\delta_{1}|p|}
for some positive constant
\delta_{1}>0
, then we have

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\left(h_{1},\chi\right),h_{3}\right\rangle\right|+\left|\left\langle\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\left(\chi,h_{1}\right),h_{3}\right\rangle\right|\lesssim\left\|h_{3}\right\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}\left\|h_{1}\right\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}},

where the constants are independent of
\mathfrak{c}
.

We point out that Lemma
6.2
has been proved in
[
46
]
when
\mathfrak{c}=1
. For the general case, the proof is very similar to the one in
[
46
]
and we omit the details here for brevity.

To establish the uniform in
\mathfrak{c}
and
\varepsilon
estimates for the remainder
F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}_{R}
, we shall use the
L^{2}-L^{\infty}
framework from
[
24
]
. We first consider the
L^{2}
estimate.

Lemma 6.3

(
L^{2}
Estimate)
.

Let
(n_{0}(t,x),u(t,x),T_{0}(t,x))
be the smooth solution to the relativistic Euler equations (
3.1
) generated by Lemma
3.1
. Let
\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(n_{0},u,T_{0};p)
,
f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}
,
h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}
be defined in (
1.12
), (
6.1
) and (
6.2
), respectively, and let
\zeta_{0}>0
be the positive constant in Proposition
4.13
. Then there exist constants
\varepsilon_{0}>0
and
C>0
, such that for all
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
, it holds

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\right\|_{2}^{2}(t)+\frac{\zeta_{0}}{2\varepsilon}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\right\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}(t)\leq C\Big\{\sqrt{\varepsilon}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\infty,\ell}(t)+1\Big\}\left\{\left\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\right\|_{2}\right\},

(6.5)

where the constant
C
depends upon the
L^{2}
norms and the
L^{\infty}
norms of the terms
\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},F_{1}^{\mathfrak{c}},\ldots,F^{\mathfrak{c}}_{2k-1}
as well as their first derivatives, and
C
is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
.

Proof.

Plugging
F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}_{R}=f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}
into (
1.2
), one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}=-\frac{\{\partial_{t}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}\}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\varepsilon^{k-1}\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}(f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\Big\{\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big)+\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)\Big\}+\varepsilon^{k}\bar{A},

(6.6)

where

\displaystyle\bar{A}:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j\geq 2k+1\\
2\leq i,j\leq 2k-1\end{subarray}}\varepsilon^{i+j-1-2k}\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}},\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big).

Multiplying (
6
) by
f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, one has

\displaystyle\big\langle\partial_{t}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\big\rangle=-\Big\langle\Big(\frac{\left\{\partial_{t}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}\right\}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big\rangle+\langle\varepsilon^{k-1}\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}(f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}),f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Big\langle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\Big\{\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big)+\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)\Big\},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big\rangle+\langle\varepsilon^{k}\bar{A},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\rangle.

It follows from Proposition
4.13
that

\displaystyle\big\langle\partial_{t}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathbf{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\big\rangle\geq\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\zeta_{0}}{\varepsilon}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\right\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}.

For
\partial=\partial_{t}
or
\partial=\partial_{x_{i}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}=\frac{\partial n_{0}}{n_{0}}-3\frac{\partial T_{0}}{T_{0}}+\frac{\partial T_{0}}{T^{2}_{0}}\Big(u^{0}p^{0}-\mathfrak{c}^{2}\frac{K_{1}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big)-\frac{\partial T_{0}}{T^{2}_{0}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}u_{i}p_{i}+\frac{1}{T_{0}}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{3}p_{i}\partial u_{i}-\frac{\partial u\cdot u}{u^{0}}p^{0}\Big).

(6.7)

A direct calculation shows that

\Big|u^{0}p^{0}-\mathfrak{c}^{2}\frac{K_{1}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}\Big|\lesssim(1+|p|)^{2}C(n_{0},u,T_{0}),

which, together with (
6.7
), yields that

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{\left\{\partial_{t}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}\right\}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big|\lesssim(1+|p|)^{3}C(n_{0},u,T_{0}).

For any
0<\sqrt{\varepsilon}\leq\kappa
, we obtain

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\Big(\frac{\left\{\partial_{t}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}\right\}\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\int_{\{1+|p|\geq\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\}}dxdp\Big|+\Big|\int_{\{1+|p|\leq\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\}}dxdp\Big|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\kappa}\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}(n_{0},u,T_{0})\|_{2}\cdot\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\|\nabla_{x}(n_{0},u,T_{0})\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdot\|(1+|p|)^{\frac{3}{2}}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\mathbf{1}_{\{1+|p|\leq\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\kappa}\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{2}+C\|(1+|p|)^{\frac{3}{2}}\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\mathbf{1}_{\{1+|p|\leq\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\|(1+|p|)^{\frac{3}{2}}\{\mathbf{I-P_{\mathfrak{c}}}\}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\mathbf{1}_{\{1+|p|\leq\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\kappa}\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{2}+C\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{C\kappa^{2}}{\varepsilon}\|\{\mathbf{I-P_{\mathfrak{c}}}\}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}.

It follows from Lemma
6.2
that

\displaystyle|\langle\varepsilon^{k-1}\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}(f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}),f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\rangle|\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-1}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{2}\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-1}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{2}

and

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\Big\{\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big)+\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big)\Big\},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}+\|\{\mathbf{I-P_{\mathfrak{c}}}\}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{2}+\|\{\mathbf{I-P_{\mathfrak{c}}}\}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}.

Similarly, for the last term, one has

\displaystyle\Big|\Big\langle\varepsilon^{k}\bar{A},f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j\geq 2k+1\\
2\leq i,j\leq 2k-1\end{subarray}}\varepsilon^{i+j-1-2k}\Big|\Big\langle\Gamma_{\mathfrak{c}}\Big(\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}},\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}}{\sqrt{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big),f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\Big\rangle\Big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{2}\lesssim\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{2}.

Collecting all the above estimates, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{\zeta_{0}}{\varepsilon}\left\|\{\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P}_{\mathfrak{c}}\}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\right\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}\leq C_{\kappa}\varepsilon^{2}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{2}+C\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{2}+C\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+C\Big(\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\varepsilon}+1\Big)\|\{\mathbf{I-P_{\mathfrak{c}}}\}f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{k-1}\|h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\|^{2}_{2}.

We choose
\kappa=\sqrt{\frac{\zeta_{0}}{4C}}
, then we suppose that
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\frac{\zeta_{0}}{4C}
. Thus one gets (
6.5
). Therefore the proof is completed.
∎

Next we consider the
L^{\infty}
estimate for
h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}
. Recall
J_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)
in (
1.26
). We define

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{\mathfrak{c}}h:=-J_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h)+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})\}=\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}h-\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}h,

where
\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}=\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}2}-\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}1}
. More specifically,
\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}
is defined in (
1.25
) and operators
\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}1}h
and
\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}2}h
are defined as

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}1}h

\displaystyle:=J_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\Big\{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)}\frac{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)}}h(q)\Big\}d\omega dq,

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}2}h

\displaystyle:=J_{\mathfrak{c}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}^{+}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h)+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}^{+}(\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h,\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})\right\}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\Big\{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(p^{\prime})\frac{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}(q^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)}}h(q^{\prime})\Big\}d\omega dq+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}\Big\{\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(q^{\prime})\frac{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}(p^{\prime})}}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)}}h(p^{\prime})\Big\}d\omega dq.

Noting (
1.28
), by similar arguments as in
[
47
]
, one can show that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}i}(h)|\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}_{i}(p,q)|h(q)|dq,\quad i=1,2,

where

\displaystyle\hat{k}_{1}(p,q)=|p-q|e^{-\delta_{2}|p|}e^{-\delta_{2}|q|},\quad\hat{k}_{2}(p,q)=\frac{1}{|p-q|}e^{-\frac{\delta_{2}}{2}|p-q|}

with
\delta_{2}:=\alpha-\frac{1}{2}>0
. We denote
\hat{k}(p,q):=\hat{k}_{1}(p,q)+\hat{k}_{2}(p,q)
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}(h)|\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}(p,q)|h(q)|dq,\quad i=1,2.

Denote

\displaystyle\hat{k}_{w}(p,q):=\hat{k}(p,q)\frac{w_{\ell}(p)}{w_{\ell}(q)}.

By similar arguments as in Lemmas
4.4
-
4.5
, one has

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)e^{\frac{\delta_{2}}{4}|p-q|}dq+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}^{2}_{w}(p,q)dq\lesssim\max{\Big\{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}},\frac{1}{1+|p|}\Big\}}.

(6.8)

For later use, we introduce

\displaystyle\widehat{\nu}_{\mathfrak{c}}(p):=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}v_{\phi}J_{\mathfrak{c}}(q)d\omega dq\cong\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p).

Lemma 6.4

(
L^{\infty}
Estimate)
.

Under the assumptions of Lemma
6.3
, there exist
\varepsilon_{0}>0
and a positive constant
C>0
, such that for all
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0}]
and for any
\ell\geq 9
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}\leq C\Big(\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{0}\|_{\infty,\ell}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{2}+\varepsilon^{k+\frac{5}{2}}\Big),

where
C
is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
.

Proof.

Plugging
F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}_{R}=h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}
into (
1.2
), one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\hat{p}\cdot\nabla_{x}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}+\frac{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}{\varepsilon}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\mathcal{K}(h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}})+\varepsilon^{k-1}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})\Big\}+\varepsilon^{k}\tilde{A},

(6.9)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{A}:=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j\geq 2k+1\\
2\leq i,j\leq 2k-1\end{subarray}}\varepsilon^{i+j-1-2k}\frac{1}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}).

Denote
y_{1}:=x-\hat{p}(t-s)
and

\displaystyle\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s):=\int_{s}^{t}\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})(\tau,x-\hat{p}(t-\tau),p)d\tau\cong(t-s)\widehat{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}.

Integrating (
6
) along the backward trajectory, one has

\displaystyle h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)

\displaystyle=\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,0)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}h_{0}(x-\hat{p}t,p)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s,y_{1},p)ds

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\frac{\varepsilon^{k-1}}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}},h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}})(s,y_{1},p)ds

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})\Big\}(s,y_{1},p)ds

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\varepsilon^{k}\tilde{A}(s,y_{1},p)ds

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{5}\mathcal{J}_{j}.

(6.10)

It is clear that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}\mathcal{J}_{1}|\leq\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{0}\|_{\infty,\ell}.

For
\mathcal{J}_{3}
, it follows from Lemma
6.1
that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}\mathcal{J}_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\Big|\frac{w_{\ell}}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}},h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}})(s,y_{1},p)\Big|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-\frac{5}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\widehat{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(p)ds\cdot\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|^{2}_{\infty,\ell}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|^{2}_{\infty,\ell}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}\mathcal{J}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\Big|\frac{w_{\ell}}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})\Big\}(s,y_{1},p)\Big|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\widehat{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(p)ds\cdot\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\Big\|\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}(s)}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big\|_{\infty,\ell}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}\mathcal{J}_{5}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k+\frac{3}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j\geq 2k+1\\
2\leq i,j\leq 2k-1\end{subarray}}\varepsilon^{i+j-1-2k}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\Big|\frac{w_{\ell}}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})\Big|ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k+\frac{3}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\widehat{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(p)ds\cdot\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\Big\|\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}(s)}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big\|_{\infty,\ell}\cdot\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\Big\|\frac{F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}}(s)}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big\|_{\infty,\ell}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k+\frac{5}{2}}.

Collecting the above estimates, we have established

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}+C\varepsilon^{k-\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\varepsilon^{k+\frac{5}{2}}+C\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{0}\|_{\infty,\ell}+Cw_{\ell}(p)\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}|\mathcal{J}_{2}|.

(6.11)

To bound the last term
\mathcal{J}_{2}
, we denote
y_{2}:=y_{1}-\hat{q}\left(s-s^{\prime}\right)=x-\hat{p}(t-s)-\hat{q}\left(s-s^{\prime}\right)
and

\displaystyle\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime}):=\int_{s^{\prime}}^{s}\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}})(\tau,y_{1}-\hat{q}(s-\tau),q)d\tau\cong(s-s^{\prime})\widehat{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}.

We substitute (
6
) into
\mathcal{J}_{2}
to obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{J}_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}(p,q)|h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s,y_{1},q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}(p,q)\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,0)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}|h_{0}(y_{1}-\hat{q}s,q)|dq

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}(p,q)\big|\mathcal{K}_{\mathfrak{c}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q)\big|dq

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{k-2}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}(p,q)\frac{1}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big|Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}},h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}})(s^{\prime},y_{2},q)\Big|dq

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}(p,q)\frac{1}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big|\Big\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})\Big\}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q)\Big|dq

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{k-1}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds^{\prime}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}(p,q)|\tilde{A}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q)|dq

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{5}\mathcal{J}_{2j}.

By Lemma
4.6
, there exists a positive constant
\nu_{0}
which is independent of
\mathfrak{c}
, such that

\displaystyle\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)\geq\nu_{0},\quad p\in\mathbb{R}^{3}.

For
\mathcal{J}_{21}
, one has from (
6.8
) that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(p)\mathcal{J}_{21}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\nu_{0}t}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(q)h_{0}(y_{1}-\hat{q}s,q)|dq

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{0}\|_{\infty,\ell}.

Similarly, using Lemma
6.1
, we get

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(p)\mathcal{J}_{23}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)dq

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\frac{w_{\ell}(q)}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big|Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}},h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}})(s^{\prime},y_{2},q)\Big|ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k-\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|^{2}_{\infty,\ell}

and

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(p)\mathcal{J}_{24}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{2k-1}\varepsilon^{i-1}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)dq

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\frac{w_{\ell}(q)}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\Big|\Big\{Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}})+Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})\Big\}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q)\Big|ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}.

For
\mathcal{J}_{25}
, one has

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(p)\mathcal{J}_{25}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k+\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i+j\geq 2k+1\\
2\leq i,j\leq 2k-1\end{subarray}}\varepsilon^{i+j-1-2k}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\nu_{0}(t-s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)dq

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}\frac{w_{\ell}(q)}{\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}}}|Q_{\mathfrak{c}}(F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}},F_{i}^{\mathfrak{c}})(s^{\prime},y_{2},q)|ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon^{k+\frac{5}{2}}.

Now we focus on the estimate of
\mathcal{J}_{22}
. It holds that

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(p)\mathcal{J}_{22}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)dq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{k}_{w}(q,q^{\prime})|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(q^{\prime})h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q^{\prime})dq^{\prime}.

We divide the estimate into four cases.

Case 1
:
|p|\geq N
. Using (
6.8
), one has

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(p)\mathcal{J}_{22}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max{\Big\{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}},\frac{1}{1+|p|}\Big\}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max{\Big\{\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}},\frac{1}{N}\Big\}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}.

Case 2
:
|p|\leq N
,
|q|\geq 2N
or
|q|\leq 2N
,
|q^{\prime}|\geq 3N
. Using (
6.8
) again, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{0}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big\{\iint_{|p|\leq N,|q|\geq 2N}+\iint_{|q|\leq 2N,|q^{\prime}|\geq 3N}\Big\}

\displaystyle\lesssim e^{-\frac{\delta_{2}}{4}N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}\lesssim\frac{1}{N}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}.

Case 3
: For
s-s^{\prime}\leq\kappa\varepsilon
and
|p|\leq N
,
|q|\leq 2N
,
|q^{\prime}|\leq 3N
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{s-\kappa\varepsilon}^{s}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{|q|\leq 2N}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)dq\int_{|q^{\prime}|\leq 3N}\hat{k}_{2}(q,q^{\prime})|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(q^{\prime})h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q^{\prime})|dq^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}.

Case 4
: For
s-s^{\prime}\geq\kappa\varepsilon
and
|p|\leq N
,
|q|\leq 2N
,
|q^{\prime}|\leq 3N
, this is the last remaining case. Using (
6.8
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{|q|\leq 2N}\int_{|q^{\prime}|\leq 3N}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)\hat{k}_{w}(q,q^{\prime})|w_{\ell}(q^{\prime})h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q^{\prime})|dqdq^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C_{N}\int_{|q|\leq 2N}\int_{|q^{\prime}|\leq 3N}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)\hat{k}_{w}(q,q^{\prime})|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q^{\prime})|dqdq^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C_{N}\Big(\int_{|q|\leq 2N}\int_{|q^{\prime}|\leq 3N}\hat{k}^{2}_{w}(p,q)\hat{k}^{2}_{w}(q,q^{\prime})dqdq^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\Big(\int_{|q|\leq 2N}\int_{|q^{\prime}|\leq 3N}|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q^{\prime})|^{2}dqdq^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{N}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q^{\prime})|^{2}\cdot\varepsilon^{-3}\kappa^{-3}dy_{2}dq^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{N,\kappa}}{\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{2},

where we have made a change of variables
q\mapsto y_{2}
with

\displaystyle\Big|\frac{dy_{2}}{dq}\Big|=\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{5}}{(q^{0})^{5}}(s-s^{\prime})^{3}\geq\frac{\kappa^{3}\varepsilon^{3}}{3^{5}}.

Here we take
1\leq N\leq\mathfrak{c}
. Thus we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}(t,s)}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds\int_{0}^{s-\kappa\varepsilon}\exp{\Big(-\frac{\tilde{\nu_{\mathfrak{c}}}^{\prime}(s,s^{\prime})}{\varepsilon}\Big)}ds^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{|q|\leq 2N}\hat{k}_{w}(p,q)dq\int_{|q^{\prime}|\leq 3N}\hat{k}_{2}(q,q^{\prime})|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(q^{\prime})h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s^{\prime},y_{2},q^{\prime})|dq^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C_{N,\kappa}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{2}.

Collecting all the four cases, we obtain

\displaystyle|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}w_{\ell}(p)\mathcal{J}_{22}|

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\kappa+\frac{1}{N}\Big)\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}+C_{N,\kappa}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{2}.

(6.12)

Therefore, combining (
6
) and (
6.12
), one obtains

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\varepsilon+\kappa+\frac{1}{N}\Big)\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}+C\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{0}\|_{\infty,\ell}

\displaystyle\qquad+C\varepsilon^{k-\frac{3}{2}}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}^{2}+C\varepsilon^{k+\frac{5}{2}}+C_{N,\kappa}\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{2}.

(6.13)

Choosing
N
suitably large and
\kappa
,
\varepsilon
suitably small, one gets from (
6
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{\infty,\ell}\leq C\|\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}h_{0}\|_{\infty,\ell}+C\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\|f_{R}^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(s)\|_{2}+C\varepsilon^{k+\frac{5}{2}}.

Therefore the proof of Lemma
6.4
is completed.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

With Lemmas
6.3
–
6.4
in hand, the rest proof is the same as
[
25
,
46
]
. We omit the details here for brevity. Therefore the proof of Theorem
1.1
is completed.
∎

Using Theorem
1.1
, we can prove Theorem
1.5
as follows.

Proof of Theorem
1.5
.

Recall
\bar{c}_{1}
and
\bar{c}_{2}
in (
3.45
).
Using (
1.29
), for any
(t,x,p)\in[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, one has

\displaystyle|F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)-\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)|\lesssim\varepsilon\sqrt{J_{\mathfrak{c}}(p)}\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{|p|}{2T_{M}}}.

(6.14)

A direct calculation shows that

\displaystyle\mu(t,x,p)-\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big\}}-\frac{n_{0}\gamma}{4\pi\mathfrak{c}^{3}K_{2}(\gamma)}\exp\Big\{\frac{u^{\mu}p_{\mu}}{T_{0}}\Big\}

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big\}}-\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp\Big\{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+u^{\mu}p_{\mu}}{T_{0}}\Big\}(1+O(\gamma^{-1}))

\displaystyle=O(\gamma^{-1})\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp\Big\{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+u^{\mu}p_{\mu}}{T_{0}}\Big\}+\Big(\frac{\rho}{(2\pi\theta)^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big\}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big(\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big\}}-\exp\Big\{\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+u^{\mu}p_{\mu}}{T_{0}}\Big\}\Big)

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{A}_{1}+\mathcal{A}_{2}+\mathcal{A}_{3}.

(6.15)

It follows from Proposition (
3.8
) that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{1}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}e^{-2\bar{c}_{1}|p|},\quad|\mathcal{A}_{2}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}e^{-\bar{c}_{2}|p|}.

For
\mathcal{A}_{3}
, if
|p|\geq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
, one has

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p|^{2}}{4\theta}\Big\}}+\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p|}{2T_{0}}\Big\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp{\Big\{-\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{4}}}{8\theta}\Big\}}\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p|^{2}}{8\theta}\Big\}}+\exp{\Big\{-\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}}{4T_{0}}\Big\}}\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p|}{4T_{0}}\Big\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}\big(e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{2}}{2}|p|}+e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|}\big).

If
|p|\leq\mathfrak{c}^{\frac{1}{8}}
, it follows from (
4.1
)-(
4.56
) that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{A}_{3}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n_{0}}{(2\pi T_{0})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\exp{\Big\{-\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}\Big\}}\Big|1-\exp\Big\{\frac{|p-\mathfrak{u}|^{2}}{2\theta}+\frac{\mathfrak{c}^{2}+u^{\mu}p_{\mu}}{T_{0}}\Big\}\Big|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}e^{-\bar{c}_{2}|p|}.

(6.16)

Combining (
6
)–(
6.16
), one has

\displaystyle|\mu(t,x,p)-\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}(t,x,p)|\lesssim\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}(e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{2}}{2}|p|}+e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|}).

(6.17)

Using (
6.14
), (
6.17
) and taking

\displaystyle\delta_{0}:=\min{\Big(\frac{1}{2T_{M}},\,\bar{c}_{1},\,\frac{\bar{c}_{2}}{2}\Big)}>0,

one has

\displaystyle|F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}(t)-\mu(t)|\lesssim\varepsilon e^{-\frac{|p|}{2T_{M}}}+\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}(e^{-\frac{\bar{c}_{2}}{2}|p|}+e^{-\bar{c}_{1}|p|})\lesssim(\varepsilon+\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}})e^{-\delta_{0}|p|},

which implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\Big\|\big(F^{\varepsilon,\mathfrak{c}}-\mu\big)(t)e^{\delta_{0}|p|}\Big\|_{\infty}\lesssim\varepsilon+\mathfrak{c}^{-\frac{3}{2}}.

Therefore the proof of Theorem
1.5
is completed.
∎

7.
Appendix: Derivation of the orthonormal basis of
\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}

In this part, we derive the orthonormal basis of
\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}
. One needs to use (
1.13
)–(
1.14
) and Lemma
4.11
frequently. Suppose that

\displaystyle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{0}=\mathfrak{a}_{0}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}},\quad\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{j}=\frac{p_{j}-\mathfrak{a}_{j}}{\mathfrak{b}_{j}}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}}\ (j=1,2,3),\quad\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4}=\frac{p^{0}/\mathfrak{c}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}p_{i}+\mathfrak{e}}{\zeta}\mathbf{\sqrt{M_{\mathfrak{c}}}}

form an orthonormal basis of
\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}
. Using
\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{0},\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{0}\rangle=1
, one has
\mathfrak{a}_{0}=\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp\Big)^{-\frac{1}{2}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{I^{0}}}
. To compute
\mathfrak{a}_{j}
, since
\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{0},\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{j}\rangle=0
, we have

\displaystyle 0=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(p_{j}-\mathfrak{a}_{j})\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp=T^{0j}-\mathfrak{a}_{j}I^{0},

which yields that
\mathfrak{a}_{j}=\frac{T^{0j}}{I^{0}}
. For
\mathfrak{b}_{j}
, using
\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{j},\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{j}\rangle=1
, one has

\displaystyle\mathfrak{b}_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(p_{j}-\mathfrak{a}_{j})^{2}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(p^{2}_{j}+\mathfrak{a}_{j}^{2}-2\mathfrak{a}_{j}p_{j})\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=T^{0jj}+\mathfrak{a}_{j}^{2}I^{0}-2\mathfrak{a}_{j}T^{0j}=T^{0jj}-\frac{(T^{0j})^{2}}{I^{0}},

which yields that
\mathfrak{b}_{j}=\sqrt{T^{0jj}-\frac{(T^{0j})^{2}}{I^{0}}}
,
j=1,2,3
.

To determine the coefficients
\lambda_{i}
,
i=1,2,3
, due to
\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4},\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{0}\rangle=\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4},\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{j}\rangle=0
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(p^{0}/\mathfrak{c}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}p_{i}+\mathfrak{e})\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(p^{0}/\mathfrak{c}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}p_{i}+\mathfrak{e})(p_{j}-\mathfrak{a}_{j})\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=0,\ j=1,2,3.

That is

\displaystyle\frac{T^{00}}{\mathfrak{c}}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}T^{0i}+\mathfrak{e}I^{0}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\frac{T^{00j}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\frac{\mathfrak{a}_{j}}{\mathfrak{c}}T^{00}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}(T^{0ij}-\mathfrak{a}_{j}T^{0i})+\mathfrak{e}(T^{0j}-\mathfrak{a}_{j}I^{0})

\displaystyle=0,\ j=1,2,3.

One can rewrite the above linear system as

\left(\begin{array}[]{cccc}\vskip 4.64996ptT^{01}&T^{02}&T^{03}&I^{0}\\
\vskip 4.64996ptT^{011}-\mathfrak{a}_{1}T^{01}&T^{021}-\mathfrak{a}_{1}T^{02}&T^{031}-\mathfrak{a}_{1}T^{03}&T^{01}-\mathfrak{a}_{1}I^{0}\\
\vskip 4.64996ptT^{012}-\mathfrak{a}_{2}T^{01}&T^{022}-\mathfrak{a}_{2}T^{02}&T^{032}-\mathfrak{a}_{2}T^{03}&T^{02}-\mathfrak{a}_{2}I^{0}\\
\vskip 4.64996ptT^{013}-\mathfrak{a}_{3}T^{01}&T^{023}-\mathfrak{a}_{3}T^{02}&T^{033}-\mathfrak{a}_{3}T^{03}&T^{03}-\mathfrak{a}_{3}I^{0}\end{array}\right)\begin{pmatrix}\vskip 4.64996pt\lambda_{1}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\lambda_{2}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\lambda_{3}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\mathfrak{e}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\vskip 4.64996pt-\frac{T^{00}}{\mathfrak{c}}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\frac{\mathfrak{a}_{1}T^{00}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\frac{T^{001}}{\mathfrak{c}}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\frac{\mathfrak{a}_{2}T^{00}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\frac{T^{002}}{\mathfrak{c}}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\frac{\mathfrak{a}_{3}T^{00}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\frac{T^{003}}{\mathfrak{c}}\end{pmatrix}.

(7.1)

Denote

\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}:=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)},\quad\mathfrak{b}:=\frac{n_{0}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)}(6K_{3}(\gamma)+\gamma K_{2}(\gamma)).

By a tedious calculation, one can transform (
7.1
) into the following system

\displaystyle\left(\begin{array}[]{cccc}\vskip 4.64996pt0&0&0&\mathfrak{a}\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}-\mathfrak{a}\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}})\frac{|u|^{2}}{T_{0}}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\mathfrak{a}T_{0}&0&0&\mathfrak{a}\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}})u_{1}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt0&\mathfrak{a}T_{0}&0&\mathfrak{a}\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}})u_{2}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt0&0&\mathfrak{a}T_{0}&\mathfrak{a}\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}})u_{3}\\
\end{array}\right)\begin{pmatrix}\vskip 5.81247pt\lambda_{1}\\
\vskip 5.81247pt\lambda_{2}\\
\vskip 5.81247pt\lambda_{3}\\
\vskip 5.81247pt\mathfrak{e}\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}\vskip 4.64996pt\frac{n_{0}}{\gamma}-\frac{\mathfrak{a}u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\frac{n_{0}}{\gamma}(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}})\frac{|u|^{2}}{T_{0}}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\frac{n_{0}}{\gamma}(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}})u_{1}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\frac{n_{0}}{\gamma}(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}})u_{2}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\frac{n_{0}}{\gamma}(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}})u_{3}\end{pmatrix}.

Observing (
5.8
), one has
\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{\mathfrak{b}}{\mathfrak{a}}<0
, which implies that (
7
) has a unique solution. More precisely, we can write down it explicitly

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}\vskip 4.64996pt\lambda_{1}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\lambda_{2}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\lambda_{3}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\mathfrak{e}\end{pmatrix}=\frac{1}{\frac{u^{0}}{\mathfrak{c}}-\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{6}{\gamma}-\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}\Big)\frac{u^{0}|u|^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}T_{0}}}\begin{pmatrix}\vskip 4.64996pt\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{6}{\gamma}-\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}\Big)\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}T_{0}}u_{1}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{6}{\gamma}-\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}\Big)\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}T_{0}}u_{2}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{6}{\gamma}-\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}\Big)\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}T_{0}}u_{3}\\
\vskip 4.64996pt\frac{1}{\gamma}-\frac{(u^{0})^{2}}{\gamma T_{0}}\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\Big(\frac{K_{3}(\gamma)}{K_{2}(\gamma)}-\frac{6}{\gamma}-\frac{K_{2}(\gamma)}{K_{3}(\gamma)}\Big)\frac{|u|^{2}}{\gamma T_{0}}\end{pmatrix}.

For
\zeta
, it follow from
\langle\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4},\chi^{\mathfrak{c}}_{4}\rangle=1
that

\displaystyle\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(p^{0}/\mathfrak{c}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}p_{i}+\mathfrak{e})^{2}\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\frac{(p^{0})^{2}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}+\mathfrak{e}^{2}+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}\lambda_{j}p_{i}p_{j}+2\frac{\mathfrak{e}}{\mathfrak{c}}p^{0}+2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}\mathfrak{e}p_{i}+2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}}p^{0}p_{i})\mathbf{M}_{\mathfrak{c}}dp

\displaystyle=\frac{T^{000}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}+\mathfrak{e}^{2}I^{0}+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}\lambda_{j}T^{0ij}+2\frac{\mathfrak{e}}{\mathfrak{c}}T^{00}+2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}\mathfrak{e}T^{0i}+2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}}T^{00i},

which yields that

\displaystyle\zeta=\sqrt{\frac{T^{000}}{\mathfrak{c}^{2}}+\mathfrak{e}^{2}I^{0}+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}\lambda_{j}T^{0ij}+2\frac{\mathfrak{e}}{\mathfrak{c}}T^{00}+2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\lambda_{i}\mathfrak{e}T^{0i}+2\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{\lambda_{i}}{\mathfrak{c}}T^{00i}}.

Consequently, we obtain the desired orthonormal basis of
\mathcal{N}_{\mathfrak{c}}
.
